# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import time
import logging
import random
import threading
import cv2
import numpy as np
from functools import wraps
from datetime import datetime

# Airtest 核心类
from airtest.core.api import Template, wait, sleep
from airtest.core.android.android import Android
from airtest.core.error import TargetNotFoundError
from airtest.core.helper import G
# 👇 【新增】引入这个常量，为了拿我们在 KSAS.py 里设置好的路径
from airtest.core.android import constant
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader

class TaskInterruptedError(Exception):
    """任务被用户强制中断"""
    pass

# ==========================================
# 🛠️ 实例操作器
# ==========================================
class DeviceOperator:
    def __init__(self, serial, instance_id, data_dir_root="data", interrupt_callback=None):
        self.serial = serial
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{instance_id}.Device")
        
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_dir_root, instance_id)
        self.snapshot_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        os.makedirs(self.snapshot_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 🔒 实例级锁
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        
        # ⏹️ 中断检查回调 (如果返回 True，说明需要立即停止)
        self.interrupt_callback = interrupt_callback

        self.adb = None
        self._is_connected = False

        # 👇 【新增 2】 加载核心安全资源 (替换 "123456" 为你在 KeyAuth 后台的真实 File ID)
        # 这张图就是 home_check.png
        self.IMG_HOME_CHECK = ResourceLoader().get_template("209805", threshold=0.9)

        # 如果下载失败，给个警告（或者直接抛异常）
        if self.IMG_HOME_CHECK is None:
            self.logger.error("⚠️ 核心安全资源加载失败！脚本可能无法判断主城状态。")

    # ==========================================
    # 🛡️ 装饰器
    # ==========================================
    def _safe_wrap(func):
        @wraps(func)
        def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
            action_name = func.__name__
            with self.lock:
                # 1. 动作前检查中断
                self.check_interrupt()

                if not self._is_connected or not self.adb:
                    self.connect()

                try:
                    result = func(self, *args, **kwargs)
                    return True if result is None else result
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    # 中断信号直接往上抛，给 Scheduler 捕获
                    raise
                except TargetNotFoundError:
                    self.logger.warning(f"[{action_name}] 未找到目标")
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_404")
                    return False
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"[{action_name}] 异常: {e}")
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_error")
                    return False
        return wrapper

    # ==========================================
    # 🔌 系统连接
    # ==========================================
    def check_interrupt(self):
        """检查是否应该立即停止"""
        if self.interrupt_callback and self.interrupt_callback():
            self.logger.warning(f"检测到中断信号 (Callback returned True)，强制停止操作！")
            raise TaskInterruptedError("Task interrupted by user")

    def connect(self):
        with self.lock:
            try:
                # 👇 【核心修改】这里不再只传 serial，而是把 adb_path 塞进去！
                # 这样 Airtest 就绝对不敢用别人的 ADB 了
                self.adb = Android(self.serial, adb_path=constant.ADB_PATH)
                
                self._is_connected = True
                self.logger.info(f"设备对象已挂载: {self.serial}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"设备挂载失败: {e}")
                self._is_connected = False
                return False

    def _save_snapshot(self, tag):
        try:
            if not self.adb: return None
            timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
            filename = f"{timestamp}_{tag}.png"
            filepath = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, filename)
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=filepath)
            return filepath
        except:
            return None

    def snapshot(self):
        """
        兼容性方法：返回截图路径
        """
        return self._save_snapshot("temp")

    # ==========================================
    # 🔍 内部辅助：图片匹配
    # ==========================================
    def _find_template_pos(self, target):
        """
        手动实现图片匹配，兼容多种返回格式
        """
        try:
            # 1. 截取当前屏幕
            temp_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, "tmp_match.png")
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=temp_path)
            
            # 2. 读取图片
            screen = cv2.imread(temp_path)
            if screen is None: return None

            # 3. 匹配
            res = target.match_in(screen)
            
            # 4. 【核心修复】兼容性处理
            if res:
                # 情况 A: 如果是字典 (Airtest 标准返回)，取 result 字段
                if isinstance(res, dict):
                    return res.get('result')
                # 情况 B: 如果直接是元组/列表 (坐标)，直接返回
                elif isinstance(res, (tuple, list)):
                    return res
                # 情况 C: 其他奇怪的对象，尝试直接用
                else:
                    return res
            
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            # 这里打印详细一点，方便调试
            self.logger.error(f"图片匹配异常: {e} (Type: {type(res) if 'res' in locals() else 'Unknown'})")
            return None

    # ==========================================
    # 👆 动作执行
    # ==========================================
    @_safe_wrap
    def click(self, target, times=1, offset=(0, 0)):
        pos = target
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            found = False
            for _ in range(3):
                self.check_interrupt() # 每次重试前都检查
                pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
                if pos:
                    found = True
                    break
                time.sleep(0.5)
            
            if not found:
                raise TargetNotFoundError("Template not found")
        
        for _ in range(times):
            self.check_interrupt() # 连点之间检查
            if pos:
                self.adb.touch(pos)
            if times > 1:
                time.sleep(random.uniform(0.2, 0.5))

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe(self, start, end, duration=0.5):
        """
        基础滑动方法 (兼容 Airtest swipe)
        :param start: (x, y) 起点
        :param end: (x, y) 终点
        :param duration: 滑动耗时
        """
        self.adb.swipe(start, end, duration=duration)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_screen(self, direction="up", scale=0.6, area=None):
        try:
            disp = self.adb.get_display_info()
            w, h = disp['width'], disp['height']
        except:
            w, h = 720, 1280

        if area:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, area)
            bw, bh = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
            base_x, base_y = x1, y1
        else:
            bw, bh = w, h
            base_x, base_y = 0, 0

        cx, cy = base_x + bw * 0.5, base_y + bh * 0.5
        delta_h, delta_w = bh * scale * 0.5, bw * scale * 0.5

        if direction == "up":    start, end = (cx, cy + delta_h), (cx, cy - delta_h)
        elif direction == "down": start, end = (cx, cy - delta_h), (cx, cy + delta_h)
        elif direction == "left": start, end = (cx + delta_w, cy), (cx - delta_w, cy)
        elif direction == "right": start, end = (cx - delta_w, cy), (cx + delta_w, cy)
        else: return False

        self.adb.swipe(start, end) 
        sleep(0.8)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_until_found(self, target, direction="up", scale=0.5, area=None, max_retry=5):
        for i in range(max_retry):
            self.check_interrupt() # 滑动前检查
            if self._exists_internal(target):
                return True
            self.logger.info(f"寻找目标... 滑动 #{i+1}")
            self.swipe_screen(direction=direction, scale=scale, area=area)
            time.sleep(0.5)
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def pinch(self, mode="in"):
        self.logger.warning("Pinch 动作在独立模式下暂不可用")
        pass

    @_safe_wrap
    def input_text(self, content, confirm=True):
        self.adb.text(str(content), enter=confirm)
        sleep(1)

    @_safe_wrap
    def keyevent(self, keyname):
        """
        模拟按键事件
        :param keyname: 键名 (e.g. "BACK", "HOME")
        """
        self.adb.keyevent(keyname)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def press_back(self):
        self.keyevent("BACK")

    @_safe_wrap
    def app_restart(self, package_name):
        self.adb.stop_app(package_name)
        sleep(1)
        self.adb.start_app(package_name)
        sleep(5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def exists(self, target):
        return self._exists_internal(target)

    def _exists_internal(self, target):
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
            return pos if pos else False
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def sleep(self, seconds):
        # 智能休眠：如果时间长，每隔 0.5s 检查一次中断
        end_time = time.time() + seconds
        while time.time() < end_time:
            self.check_interrupt()
            remaining = end_time - time.time()
            time.sleep(min(0.5, remaining))

    # ==========================================
    # 👁️ 数据获取
    # ==========================================
    def get_text(self, area=None):
        return ""

    def parse_number(self, text):
        if not text: return 0
        s = str(text).lower().replace(" ", "").replace(",", "")
        multiplier = 1
        if 'k' in s:
            multiplier = 1000
            s = s.replace('k', '')
        elif 'm' in s:
            multiplier = 1000000
            s = s.replace('m', '')
        try:
            val = float(s)
            return int(val * multiplier)
        except:
            return 0

    # ==========================================
    # 🏠 导航与状态
    # ==========================================
    def check_resolution(self):
        try:
            output = self.adb.shell("wm size")
            if not output: return False
            import re
            match = re.search(r"(\d+)x(\d+)", output)
            if match:
                w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
                if (w == 720 and h == 1280) or (w == 1280 and h == 720): return True
            return False
        except:
            return False

    def ensure_home(self):

        img_home = self.IMG_HOME_CHECK
        if not img_home:
            self.logger.error("核心资源缺失(Home Check)，无法执行回城逻辑！")
            return False
        # img_home = Template(r"assets/images/home/home_check.png")
        img_island = Template(r"assets/images/home/island.png")
        img_town_btn = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png")
        
        if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_island):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_town_btn):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        
        self.logger.warning("尝试暴力复位...")

        for _ in range(10):
            self.press_back()
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        return False

FILE: .\config.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import logging

class GlobalConfig:
    _instance = None
    
    DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
        "language": "zh_CN",
        "close_to_tray": False,   # 默认关闭：点击关闭按钮直接退出
        "auto_connect": True,    # 启动时自动连接
        "adb_path": "",          # 自定义 ADB 路径
        "use_internal_adb": True, # 是否使用内置 ADB
        "license_key": ""        # KeyAuth 卡密
    }

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if not cls._instance:
            cls._instance = super(GlobalConfig, cls).__new__(cls, *args, **kwargs)
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, config_path="config.json"):
        if hasattr(self, "_initialized"):
            return
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.config = self.DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("GlobalConfig")
        self.load()
        self._initialized = True

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.config_path):
            try:
                with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    # 合并配置，确保新字段存在
                    for key, val in data.items():
                        self.config[key] = val
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"加载全局配置失败: {e}")

    def save(self):
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self.config, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"保存全局配置失败: {e}")

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        return self.config.get(key, default)

    def set(self, key, value):
        self.config[key] = value
        self.save()

# 全局单例
global_config = GlobalConfig()


==================================================
FILE: .\datastore.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import threading
import logging
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

class InstanceDataStore(QObject):
    """
    单个实例的数据管家 (Per-Instance Data Manager)
    
    职责：
    1. 管理 config.json (用户配置：读多写少)
    2. 管理 statistics.json (游戏数据：写多读少)
    3. 维护 runtime memory (内存状态：不存盘)
    4. 作为 UI 和 后台脚本 的通讯枢纽 (Signals)
    """

    # ==================================================
    # 📡 信号定义 (Signals for UI)
    # ==================================================
    
    # 1. 配置变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：代码里重置了配置 -> UI 复选框自动更新
    sig_config_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 2. 统计/资源数据变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：脚本 OCR 读到了新体力 -> UI 进度条刷新
    sig_stats_updated = Signal(str, object)

    # 3. 运行时状态变更信号: (key, new_value)
    # 场景：ADB 断开连接 -> UI 状态灯变红
    sig_runtime_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 4. 日志信号: (level, message)
    # 场景：把后台的 print/log 转发到 UI 的文本框里
    sig_log_msg = Signal(str, str)

    def __init__(self, instance_id, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        
        # --- 路径配置 ---
        # 你的结构: data/deep_sl_002/
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_root, instance_id)
        self.config_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "config.json")
        self.stats_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "statistics.json")

        # --- 线程安全锁 ---
        # RLock (可重入锁) 允许同一个线程多次获得锁，防止死锁
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        # --- 内存数据容器 ---
        self._config = {}   # 对应 config.json
        self._stats = {}    # 对应 statistics.json
        self._runtime = {   # 纯内存状态 (关机即焚)
            "is_running": False,       # 调度器运行状态
            "connect_status": "OFFLINE", # ADB 连接状态
            "current_task": "Idle"     # 当前正在执行的任务名
        }

        # --- 初始化 ---
        self._ensure_dirs()
        self.load_all()

    def _ensure_dirs(self):
        """确保专属文件夹及子文件夹结构完整"""
        # 1. 创建实例主目录 (data/deep_sl_002)
        if not os.path.exists(self.base_dir):
            os.makedirs(self.base_dir, exist_ok=True)
            
        # 2. 创建日志目录 (data/deep_sl_002/log)
        log_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "log")
        if not os.path.exists(log_dir):
            os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 3. 创建截图目录 (data/deep_sl_002/snapshots)
        snap_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        if not os.path.exists(snap_dir):
            os.makedirs(snap_dir, exist_ok=True)

    def load_all(self):
        """加载所有持久化数据"""
        # 1. 加载配置 (带默认值)
        default_config = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_config.json")
        if default_config:
            # 动态设置 instance_name 默认值
            default_config["instance_name"] = self.instance_id
        else:
            # Fallback if file missing
            default_config = {"instance_name": self.instance_id}
            
        self._load_json(self.config_path, self._config, default_config)

        # 2. 加载统计 (默认为空)
        default_stats = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_statistics.json")
        if not default_stats:
             default_stats = {}
             
        self._load_json(self.stats_path, self._stats, default_stats)

    def _load_default_json(self, path):
        """从模板文件加载默认配置"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading default json {path}: {e}")
        return None

    def _load_json(self, path, container, default):
        """通用的安全读取逻辑 (带简单的二级补全)"""
        with self._lock:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        saved_data = json.load(f)
                        
                        # 1. 先把默认值深拷贝进来
                        # (这样能保证拥有最全的字段结构)
                        import copy
                        merged_data = copy.deepcopy(default)
                        
                        # 2. 递归更新 (这里只做两层简单的合并，够用了)
                        for key, val in saved_data.items():
                            if isinstance(val, dict) and key in merged_data and isinstance(merged_data[key], dict):
                                # 如果是字典（比如 modules），进去更新里面的字段
                                merged_data[key].update(val)
                            else:
                                # 如果是普通值，直接覆盖
                                merged_data[key] = val
                        
                        # 3. 更新到内存容器
                        container.clear()
                        container.update(merged_data)
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    self.push_log(f"文件 {os.path.basename(path)} 损坏/解析失败: {e}", "ERROR")
                    # 损坏了就用默认值
                    container.clear()
                    container.update(default)
                    self._save_json(path, container)
            else:
                # 文件不存在，初始化为默认值
                container.update(default)
                self._save_json(path, container)

    def _save_json(self, path, container):
        """通用的安全保存逻辑"""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 临时写入再重命名，防止写入一半断电导致文件损坏 (Atomic Write 思想)
                temp_path = path + ".tmp"
                with open(temp_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(container, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
                
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    os.remove(path)
                os.rename(temp_path, path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.push_log(f"保存 {os.path.basename(path)} 失败: {e}", "ERROR")

    def save_config(self):
        """手动触发保存配置"""
        self._save_json(self.config_path, self._config)

    def save_stats(self):
        """手动触发保存统计"""
        self._save_json(self.stats_path, self._stats)

    # ==================================================
    # 🔧 数据操作接口 (Getters & Setters)
    # ==================================================

    # --- 1. 用户配置 (Config) ---
    
    def get_cfg(self, key_path, default=None):
        """
        读取配置
        用法: get_cfg("modules.hunting.enable")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, default)

    def set_cfg(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改配置 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_cfg("modules.hunting.enable", True)
        """
        # 如果值确实改变了
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, value):
            self.save_config() # 持久化
            self.sig_config_changed.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 2. 游戏数据 (Statistics) ---
    
    def get_stat(self, key_path, default=0):
        """
        读取游戏统计
        用法: get_stat("resources.wood")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, default)

    def set_stat(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改游戏数据 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_stat("stamina", 80)
        """
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, value):
            self.save_stats() # 持久化
            self.sig_stats_updated.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 3. 运行时状态 (Runtime Memory) ---

    def get_runtime(self, key, default=None):
        """读取内存状态"""
        with self._lock:
            return self._runtime.get(key, default)

    def set_runtime(self, key, value):
        """
        修改运行时状态 -> 仅内存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_runtime("connect_status", "ONLINE")
        """
        with self._lock:
            if self._runtime.get(key) != value:
                self._runtime[key] = value
                self.sig_runtime_changed.emit(key, value) # 通知 UI

    # ==================================================
    # 🔨 辅助工具 (Helpers)
    # ==================================================

    def push_log(self, message, level="INFO"):
        """
        发送日志信号 (给 UI 显示用)
        并不负责写 log 文件 (写文件由 logging 模块在别处处理)
        """
        self.sig_log_msg.emit(level, message)

    def _get_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, default):
        """
        [内部工具] 解析点号路径: 'a.b.c' -> dict['a']['b']['c']
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                for k in keys:
                    data = data[k]
                return data
            except (KeyError, TypeError):
                return default

    def _set_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, value):
        """
        [内部工具] 设置点号路径: 'a.b.c' = value
        返回 True 表示值发生了变化 (需要保存/刷新)
        返回 False 表示值没变 (节省 IO)
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 递归找到倒数第二层
                for k in keys[:-1]:
                    # 如果中间层不存在，自动创建空字典
                    data = data.setdefault(k, {})
                
                last_key = keys[-1]
                old_value = data.get(last_key)
                
                if old_value != value:
                    data[last_key] = value
                    return True # 变了
                return False # 没变
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"设置键值失败 {path}: {e}")
                return False

# 文件结束

# KeyAuth 资源保护实施计划 (Memory Loading - Simplified)

本计划旨在通过将关键识别图片移至 KeyAuth 云端，并采用**内存加载 (Memory Loading)** 技术，构建高安全性的“无文件落地”防御体系。

## 1. 核心策略
*   **资源依赖**: 将脚本运行必不可少的 4 张关键图片上传至 KeyAuth Files。
*   **内存加载**: 客户端启动后，从云端下载图片字节流 (bytes) 直接存入内存，**绝不写入硬盘**。
*   **极简模板**: 实现 `MemoryTemplate` 类，仅重写读取逻辑，移除不必要的坐标和分辨率参数。

## 2. 保护对象 (KeyAuth Files)

需在 KeyAuth 后台上传以下图片，并记录对应的 File ID：

| 原始路径 | 资源 Key (代码引用名) | 重要性 | 作用 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `assets/images/home/home_check.png` | `img_home_check` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 脚本启动基石，判断是否在主页 |
| `assets/images/combat/formation1.png` | `img_formation` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 战斗任务入口判断 |
| `assets/images/combat/add.png` | `img_add` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 选将逻辑核心 |
| `assets/images/combat/clear_full.png` | `img_clear_full` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 兵力设置逻辑 |

## 3. 代码实施步骤

### 步骤 1: 创建 `config/resource_loader.py`
实现 `ResourceLoader` 单例类，负责：
*   **存储**: 维护一个 `_memory_cache` 字典 (key -> bytes)。
*   **下载**: `load(keyauth_app, file_id, resource_key)` 方法，将云端文件流存入字典。
*   **获取**: `get_bytes(resource_key)` 方法，返回图片的二进制数据。

### 步骤 2: 创建 `config/memory_template.py`
实现 `MemoryTemplate` 类，继承自 `airtest.core.api.Template`：
*   **构造函数**: 仅接收 `image_bytes` 和 `threshold` 等基础参数。**不强制设置 resolution 和 record_pos**。
*   **重写 `_imread`**: 使用 `cv2.imdecode` 将内存中的 `bytes` 解码为 OpenCV 图像对象，替代原有的文件读取逻辑。

### 步骤 3: 修改 `KSAS.py` (启动加载)
在登录成功后的逻辑块中：
1.  实例化 `ResourceLoader`。
2.  调用 `load()` 方法依次下载上述 4 张图片到内存。
    *   *注意*: 需处理下载失败的情况，确保核心资源就位。

### 步骤 4: 修改任务代码 (使用 MemoryTemplate)
修改 `tasks/combat/combat_task.py` 等文件：

**示例 (CombatTask):**
```python
# 原代码
IMG_FORMATION = Template(r"assets/images/combat/formation1.png", threshold=0.9)

# 新代码
from config.resource_loader import ResourceLoader
from config.memory_template import MemoryTemplate

class CombatTask(BaseTask):
    def __init__(self, ...):
        # 从内存获取数据
        img_bytes = ResourceLoader().get_bytes("img_formation")
        
        if not img_bytes: 
            raise Exception("Resource missing: formation1")
            
        # 使用 MemoryTemplate (极简调用)
        self.IMG_FORMATION = MemoryTemplate(img_bytes, threshold=0.9)
```

## 4. 调试与发布
*   **Debug 模式**: 在 `ResourceLoader` 中增加 `DEBUG` 开关。开启时，读取本地 `assets/` 文件并转为 bytes 返回，方便开发调试。
*   **发布模式**: 关闭 `DEBUG` 开关，强制从 KeyAuth 下载。

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# instance_manager.py
import os
import json
import logging
import re
import time
import shutil
from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

from datastore import InstanceDataStore
from scheduler import InstanceScheduler
from task_registry import get_all_tasks
from basic import DeviceOperator
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class InstanceManager(QObject):
    """
    实例管理器
    """
    sig_instance_list_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.data_root = data_root
        self.registry_path = os.path.join(data_root, "device_registry.json")
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("InstanceManager")

        self.data_stores = {} 
        self.schedulers = {} 
        self._registry = {}

        self._load_registry()
        self._init_all_instances()

    def _load_registry(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.registry_path):
            try:
                with open(self.registry_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    content = f.read().strip()
                    if content:
                        self._registry = json.loads(content)
                    else:
                        self._registry = {}
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_registry_corrupted", e))
                self._registry = {}
        else:
            self._registry = {}

    def _save_registry(self):
        try:
            with open(self.registry_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self._registry, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_save_registry_failed", e))

    def _init_all_instances(self):
        """启动时加载所有已注册账号"""
        task_classes = get_all_tasks()

        for instance_id, info in self._registry.items():
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_loading_instance", info.get('display_name'), instance_id))
            store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = store
            
            try:
                device = DeviceOperator(info['serial'], instance_id)
                # device.connect() # 启动时不强制连接，为了速度
                sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, store, task_classes)
                
                # 🔄 【绑定】设置 DeviceOperator 的中断回调
                # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理，避免闭包引用问题)
                # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
                
                self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched
                
                # 🧹 启动自动清理 (异步或同步均可，这里用同步简单处理，因为文件IO通常很快)
                self._cleanup_files(instance_id)
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_load_scheduler_failed", instance_id, e))

    def create_instance(self, display_name, serial, diana_level=0, march_slots=5):
        safe_name = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '_', display_name).lower()
        instance_id = safe_name
        counter = 1
        while instance_id in self._registry:
            instance_id = f"{safe_name}_{counter}"
            counter += 1

        try:
            self._registry[instance_id] = {
                "display_name": display_name,
                "serial": serial,
                "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            }
            self._save_registry()

            new_store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.serial", serial)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.alias", display_name)
            # 设置 Diana 等级和行军队列
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(diana_level))
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(march_slots))
            
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = new_store

            device = DeviceOperator(serial, instance_id)
            device.connect()
            
            task_classes = get_all_tasks()
            sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, new_store, task_classes)
            
            # 🔄 【绑定】设置中断回调
            # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理)
            # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
            
            self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched

            self.logger.info(get_text("log_instance_created", display_name))
            self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
            return True, instance_id

        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_create_instance_failed", e))
            if instance_id in self._registry:
                del self._registry[instance_id]
            return False, str(e)

    def delete_instance(self, instance_id):
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_deleting_instance", instance_id))

        if instance_id in self.schedulers:
            try:
                sched = self.schedulers[instance_id]
                if sched.isRunning():
                    sched.stop_scheduler()
                time.sleep(0.1)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_stop_scheduler_error", e))
            del self.schedulers[instance_id]

        if instance_id in self.data_stores:
            del self.data_stores[instance_id]

        dir_path = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
        if os.path.exists(dir_path):
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(dir_path)
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_folder_deleted", dir_path))
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_delete_failed", e))

        del self._registry[instance_id]
        self._save_registry()
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()

    def update_instance(self, instance_id, new_serial=None, new_alias=None, new_diana_level=None, new_march_slots=None):
        """
        更新实例信息
        """
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return False

        # 1. 更新注册表 (只存核心信息)
        if new_serial:
            self._registry[instance_id]['serial'] = new_serial
        if new_alias:
            self._registry[instance_id]['display_name'] = new_alias
        self._save_registry()

        # 2. 更新 DataStore (存配置)
        store = self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
        if store:
            if new_serial:
                store.set_cfg("meta.serial", new_serial)
                if instance_id in self.schedulers:
                    self.schedulers[instance_id].device.serial = new_serial
            if new_alias:
                store.set_cfg("meta.alias", new_alias)
            
            # 👇 【新增】保存 Diana 等级
            if new_diana_level is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(new_diana_level))

            # 👇 【新增】保存行军队列数量
            if new_march_slots is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(new_march_slots))
        
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
        return True

    def get_all_instances(self):
        result = []
        for iid, info in self._registry.items():
            store = self.data_stores.get(iid)
            status = store.get_runtime("connect_status", "OFFLINE") if store else "ERROR"
            result.append({
                "id": iid,
                "name": info["display_name"],
                "serial": info["serial"],
                "status": status
            })
        return result

    def get_store(self, instance_id):
        return self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
    
    def get_scheduler(self, instance_id):
        return self.schedulers.get(instance_id)

    def _cleanup_files(self, instance_id):
        """清理过期日志和截图 (保留7天)"""
        try:
            base_dir = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
            targets = [
                os.path.join(base_dir, "log"),
                os.path.join(base_dir, "snapshots")
            ]
            
            now = time.time()
            retention_days = 7
            retention_sec = retention_days * 86400
            
            for folder in targets:
                if not os.path.exists(folder):
                    continue
                    
                for filename in os.listdir(folder):
                    file_path = os.path.join(folder, filename)
                    # 仅处理文件
                    if os.path.isfile(file_path):
                        # 获取修改时间
                        mtime = os.path.getmtime(file_path)
                        if now - mtime > retention_sec:
                            try:
                                os.remove(file_path)
                                self.logger.info(get_text("log_cleanup_file", instance_id, filename))
                            except Exception as del_err:
                                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_cleanup_file_failed", filename, del_err))
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_cleanup_error", instance_id, e))

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys
import os
import subprocess

# 限制第三方库线程 (优化性能)
os.environ["OMP_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["MKL_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["PADDLE_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["OPENCV_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"

import logging
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from ui.main_window_logic import MainWindowLogic

from config import global_config
# 引入 Airtest 常量
from airtest.core.android import constant

# 引入 KeyAuth
from tools.keyauth.keyauth import api
from ui.login_dialog import LoginDialog
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader

def getchecksum():
    # 简单的 checksum 占位符，实际应读取文件 MD5
    return "checksum_placeholder"

def init_keyauth():
    return api(
        name = "KSAS",
        ownerid = "NVEZ7gY47U",
        version = "1.0",
        hash_to_check = getchecksum()
    )

def setup_global_adb():
    """
    设置 Airtest 全局 ADB 路径 (绝对路径 + 和平共处版)
    """
    print("-" * 30)
    print("[System] 正在初始化 ADB 设置...")
    
    use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
    custom_path = global_config.get("adb_path", "")
    
    target_adb = None
    
    # 1. 【核心修改】获取 KSAS.py 文件所在的绝对目录
    # 这确保了无论脚本在哪里运行，都能找到旁边的 tools 文件夹
    project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    
    if use_internal:
        # 拼接绝对路径
        internal_path = os.path.join(project_root, "tools", "adb", "adb.exe")
        if os.path.exists(internal_path):
            target_adb = internal_path
        else:
            print(f"❌ [System] 内置 ADB 文件未找到: {internal_path}")
            print("   (请确保 tools/adb/adb.exe 存在且结构正确)")
    elif custom_path and os.path.exists(custom_path):
        target_adb = custom_path
            
    if target_adb:
        adb_dir = os.path.dirname(target_adb)
        print(f"✅ [System] 锁定目标 ADB: {target_adb}")
        
        # 2. 【核心】修改 Airtest 内部常量
        constant.ADB_PATH = target_adb
        
        # 3. 【核心】将我们的 ADB 目录插入系统 PATH 的第一位
        # 这能拦截大部分直接调用 "adb" 命令的库
        os.environ["PATH"] = adb_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

        # 4. 【核心】修正 adbutils 库的指向 (如果有的话)
        # 这就是解决你刚才看到“指向 site-packages”问题的关键
        try:
            import adbutils
            adbutils._adb_path = target_adb
            print("[System] 已修正 adbutils 库的路径指向")
        except ImportError:
            pass 
            
    else:
        print("⚠️ [System] 未找到指定 ADB，将使用系统默认路径 (可能会导致兼容性问题)")
    
    print("-" * 30)

# 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - [%(name)s] - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
)
logger = logging.getLogger("Main")

# logging_setup.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import logging
import os
# 👇 引入轮转文件处理器
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler

class GuiLogHandler(logging.Handler):
    """
    自定义日志处理器：拦截日志并转发给 DataStore -> UI
    """
    def __init__(self, datastore):
        super().__init__()
        self.datastore = datastore
        self.instance_id = datastore.instance_id
        # 设置格式: 只取消息内容
        self.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))

    def emit(self, record):
        """
        当有日志产生时触发
        """
        try:
            msg = self.format(record)
            level = record.levelname
            # 拼装 ID，防止 UI 串台
            full_msg = f"{self.instance_id}|||{msg}"
            self.datastore.push_log(full_msg, level)
        except Exception:
            self.handleError(record)

def setup_file_logging(instance_id, log_dir):
    """
    给指定实例挂载文件日志功能 (带自动切割功能)
    :return: Handler 对象
    """
    logger = logging.getLogger(instance_id)
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    
    # 1. 检查是否已经有文件处理器了 (防止重复添加)
    for h in logger.handlers:
        if isinstance(h, RotatingFileHandler):
            return h

    # 2. 确保目录存在
    if not os.path.exists(log_dir):
        os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

    # 3. 创建轮转文件处理器 (核心修改)
    log_path = os.path.join(log_dir, "ksas.log")
    
    # maxBytes=2*1024*1024 (2MB)
    # backupCount=3 (最多保留3个旧文件，比如 ksas.log.1, ksas.log.2)
    file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
        log_path, 
        mode='a', 
        maxBytes=2*1024*1024, 
        backupCount=3, 
        encoding='utf-8'
    )
    
    # 4. 设置格式
    formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - [%(name)s] - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
    file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    
    # 5. 挂载
    logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    
    return file_handler

FILE: .\pack_project.py
==================================================
import os

# 设置要忽略的文件夹 (黑名单)
IGNORE_DIRS = {
    "venv", ".venv", "ksas_env", "__pycache__", ".git", ".idea", ".vscode", 
    "build", "dist", "paddleocr", "snapshots", "log"
}

# 设置要读取的文件后缀 (白名单)
INCLUDE_EXTS = {".py", ".json", ".ui", ".md", ".txt"}

def pack_project():
    output_file = "project_summary.txt"
    
    with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        # 1. 先写一个目录结构树
        out.write("=== PROJECT STRUCTURE ===\n")
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
            # 过滤掉黑名单目录
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            level = root.replace(os.getcwd(), '').count(os.sep)
            indent = ' ' * 4 * (level)
            out.write(f"{indent}{os.path.basename(root)}/\n")
            subindent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
            for f in files:
                out.write(f"{subindent}{f}\n")
        
        out.write("\n\n=== FILE CONTENTS ===\n")

        # 2. 遍历读取文件内容
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for filename in files:
                ext = os.path.splitext(filename)[1]
                if ext in INCLUDE_EXTS:
                    filepath = os.path.join(root, filename)
                    
                    # 写入分隔符和文件名
                    out.write(f"\n{'='*50}\n")
                    out.write(f"FILE: {filepath}\n")
                    out.write(f"{'='*50}\n")
                    
                    try:
                        with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                            content = f.read()
                            out.write(content)
                    except Exception as e:
                        out.write(f"[Error reading file: {e}]")
                    
                    out.write("\n")

    print(f"✅ 打包完成！请把生成的 '{output_file}' 发给我。")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()

FILE: .\basic.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import time
import logging
import random
import threading
import cv2
import numpy as np
from functools import wraps
from datetime import datetime

# Airtest 核心类
from airtest.core.api import Template, wait, sleep
from airtest.core.android.android import Android
from airtest.core.error import TargetNotFoundError
from airtest.core.helper import G
# 👇 【新增】引入这个常量，为了拿我们在 KSAS.py 里设置好的路径
from airtest.core.android import constant
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader

class TaskInterruptedError(Exception):
    """任务被用户强制中断"""
    pass

# ==========================================
# 🛠️ 实例操作器
# ==========================================
class DeviceOperator:
    def __init__(self, serial, instance_id, data_dir_root="data", interrupt_callback=None):
        self.serial = serial
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{instance_id}.Device")
        
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_dir_root, instance_id)
        self.snapshot_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        os.makedirs(self.snapshot_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 🔒 实例级锁
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        
        # ⏹️ 中断检查回调 (如果返回 True，说明需要立即停止)
        self.interrupt_callback = interrupt_callback

        self.adb = None
        self._is_connected = False

        # 👇 【新增 2】 加载核心安全资源 (替换 "123456" 为你在 KeyAuth 后台的真实 File ID)
        # 这张图就是 home_check.png
        self.IMG_HOME_CHECK = ResourceLoader().get_template("209805", threshold=0.9)

        # 如果下载失败，给个警告（或者直接抛异常）
        if self.IMG_HOME_CHECK is None:
            self.logger.error("⚠️ 核心安全资源加载失败！脚本可能无法判断主城状态。")

    # ==========================================
    # 🛡️ 装饰器
    # ==========================================
    def _safe_wrap(func):
        @wraps(func)
        def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
            action_name = func.__name__
            with self.lock:
                # 1. 动作前检查中断
                self.check_interrupt()

                if not self._is_connected or not self.adb:
                    self.connect()

                try:
                    result = func(self, *args, **kwargs)
                    return True if result is None else result
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    # 中断信号直接往上抛，给 Scheduler 捕获
                    raise
                except TargetNotFoundError:
                    self.logger.warning(f"[{action_name}] 未找到目标")
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_404")
                    return False
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"[{action_name}] 异常: {e}")
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_error")
                    return False
        return wrapper

    # ==========================================
    # 🔌 系统连接
    # ==========================================
    def check_interrupt(self):
        """检查是否应该立即停止"""
        if self.interrupt_callback and self.interrupt_callback():
            self.logger.warning(f"检测到中断信号 (Callback returned True)，强制停止操作！")
            raise TaskInterruptedError("Task interrupted by user")

    def connect(self):
        with self.lock:
            try:
                # 👇 【核心修改】这里不再只传 serial，而是把 adb_path 塞进去！
                # 这样 Airtest 就绝对不敢用别人的 ADB 了
                self.adb = Android(self.serial, adb_path=constant.ADB_PATH)
                
                self._is_connected = True
                self.logger.info(f"设备对象已挂载: {self.serial}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"设备挂载失败: {e}")
                self._is_connected = False
                return False

    def _save_snapshot(self, tag):
        try:
            if not self.adb: return None
            timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
            filename = f"{timestamp}_{tag}.png"
            filepath = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, filename)
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=filepath)
            return filepath
        except:
            return None

    def snapshot(self):
        """
        兼容性方法：返回截图路径
        """
        return self._save_snapshot("temp")

    # ==========================================
    # 🔍 内部辅助：图片匹配
    # ==========================================
    def _find_template_pos(self, target):
        """
        手动实现图片匹配，兼容多种返回格式
        """
        try:
            # 1. 截取当前屏幕
            temp_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, "tmp_match.png")
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=temp_path)
            
            # 2. 读取图片
            screen = cv2.imread(temp_path)
            if screen is None: return None

            # 3. 匹配
            res = target.match_in(screen)
            
            # 4. 【核心修复】兼容性处理
            if res:
                # 情况 A: 如果是字典 (Airtest 标准返回)，取 result 字段
                if isinstance(res, dict):
                    return res.get('result')
                # 情况 B: 如果直接是元组/列表 (坐标)，直接返回
                elif isinstance(res, (tuple, list)):
                    return res
                # 情况 C: 其他奇怪的对象，尝试直接用
                else:
                    return res
            
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            # 这里打印详细一点，方便调试
            self.logger.error(f"图片匹配异常: {e} (Type: {type(res) if 'res' in locals() else 'Unknown'})")
            return None

    # ==========================================
    # 👆 动作执行
    # ==========================================
    @_safe_wrap
    def click(self, target, times=1, offset=(0, 0)):
        pos = target
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            found = False
            for _ in range(3):
                self.check_interrupt() # 每次重试前都检查
                pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
                if pos:
                    found = True
                    break
                time.sleep(0.5)
            
            if not found:
                raise TargetNotFoundError("Template not found")
        
        for _ in range(times):
            self.check_interrupt() # 连点之间检查
            if pos:
                self.adb.touch(pos)
            if times > 1:
                time.sleep(random.uniform(0.2, 0.5))

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe(self, start, end, duration=0.5):
        """
        基础滑动方法 (兼容 Airtest swipe)
        :param start: (x, y) 起点
        :param end: (x, y) 终点
        :param duration: 滑动耗时
        """
        self.adb.swipe(start, end, duration=duration)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_screen(self, direction="up", scale=0.6, area=None):
        try:
            disp = self.adb.get_display_info()
            w, h = disp['width'], disp['height']
        except:
            w, h = 720, 1280

        if area:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, area)
            bw, bh = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
            base_x, base_y = x1, y1
        else:
            bw, bh = w, h
            base_x, base_y = 0, 0

        cx, cy = base_x + bw * 0.5, base_y + bh * 0.5
        delta_h, delta_w = bh * scale * 0.5, bw * scale * 0.5

        if direction == "up":    start, end = (cx, cy + delta_h), (cx, cy - delta_h)
        elif direction == "down": start, end = (cx, cy - delta_h), (cx, cy + delta_h)
        elif direction == "left": start, end = (cx + delta_w, cy), (cx - delta_w, cy)
        elif direction == "right": start, end = (cx - delta_w, cy), (cx + delta_w, cy)
        else: return False

        self.adb.swipe(start, end) 
        sleep(0.8)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_until_found(self, target, direction="up", scale=0.5, area=None, max_retry=5):
        for i in range(max_retry):
            self.check_interrupt() # 滑动前检查
            if self._exists_internal(target):
                return True
            self.logger.info(f"寻找目标... 滑动 #{i+1}")
            self.swipe_screen(direction=direction, scale=scale, area=area)
            time.sleep(0.5)
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def pinch(self, mode="in"):
        self.logger.warning("Pinch 动作在独立模式下暂不可用")
        pass

    @_safe_wrap
    def input_text(self, content, confirm=True):
        self.adb.text(str(content), enter=confirm)
        sleep(1)

    @_safe_wrap
    def keyevent(self, keyname):
        """
        模拟按键事件
        :param keyname: 键名 (e.g. "BACK", "HOME")
        """
        self.adb.keyevent(keyname)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def press_back(self):
        self.keyevent("BACK")

    @_safe_wrap
    def app_restart(self, package_name):
        self.adb.stop_app(package_name)
        sleep(1)
        self.adb.start_app(package_name)
        sleep(5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def exists(self, target):
        return self._exists_internal(target)

    def _exists_internal(self, target):
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
            return pos if pos else False
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def sleep(self, seconds):
        # 智能休眠：如果时间长，每隔 0.5s 检查一次中断
        end_time = time.time() + seconds
        while time.time() < end_time:
            self.check_interrupt()
            remaining = end_time - time.time()
            time.sleep(min(0.5, remaining))

    # ==========================================
    # 👁️ 数据获取
    # ==========================================
    def get_text(self, area=None):
        return ""

    def parse_number(self, text):
        if not text: return 0
        s = str(text).lower().replace(" ", "").replace(",", "")
        multiplier = 1
        if 'k' in s:
            multiplier = 1000
            s = s.replace('k', '')
        elif 'm' in s:
            multiplier = 1000000
            s = s.replace('m', '')
        try:
            val = float(s)
            return int(val * multiplier)
        except:
            return 0

    # ==========================================
    # 🏠 导航与状态
    # ==========================================
    def check_resolution(self):
        try:
            output = self.adb.shell("wm size")
            if not output: return False
            import re
            match = re.search(r"(\d+)x(\d+)", output)
            if match:
                w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
                if (w == 720 and h == 1280) or (w == 1280 and h == 720): return True
            return False
        except:
            return False

    def ensure_home(self):

        img_home = self.IMG_HOME_CHECK
        if not img_home:
            self.logger.error("核心资源缺失(Home Check)，无法执行回城逻辑！")
            return False
        # img_home = Template(r"assets/images/home/home_check.png")
        img_island = Template(r"assets/images/home/island.png")
        img_town_btn = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png")
        
        if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_island):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_town_btn):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        
        self.logger.warning("尝试暴力复位...")

        for _ in range(10):
            self.press_back()
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        return False

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import logging

class GlobalConfig:
    _instance = None
    
    DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
        "language": "zh_CN",
        "close_to_tray": False,   # 默认关闭：点击关闭按钮直接退出
        "auto_connect": True,    # 启动时自动连接
        "adb_path": "",          # 自定义 ADB 路径
        "use_internal_adb": True, # 是否使用内置 ADB
        "license_key": ""        # KeyAuth 卡密
    }

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if not cls._instance:
            cls._instance = super(GlobalConfig, cls).__new__(cls, *args, **kwargs)
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, config_path="config.json"):
        if hasattr(self, "_initialized"):
            return
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.config = self.DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("GlobalConfig")
        self.load()
        self._initialized = True

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.config_path):
            try:
                with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    # 合并配置，确保新字段存在
                    for key, val in data.items():
                        self.config[key] = val
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"加载全局配置失败: {e}")

    def save(self):
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self.config, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"保存全局配置失败: {e}")

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        return self.config.get(key, default)

    def set(self, key, value):
        self.config[key] = value
        self.save()

# 全局单例
global_config = GlobalConfig()

FILE: .\datastore.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import threading
import logging
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

class InstanceDataStore(QObject):
    """
    单个实例的数据管家 (Per-Instance Data Manager)
    
    职责：
    1. 管理 config.json (用户配置：读多写少)
    2. 管理 statistics.json (游戏数据：写多读少)
    3. 维护 runtime memory (内存状态：不存盘)
    4. 作为 UI 和 后台脚本 的通讯枢纽 (Signals)
    """

    # ==================================================
    # 📡 信号定义 (Signals for UI)
    # ==================================================
    
    # 1. 配置变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：代码里重置了配置 -> UI 复选框自动更新
    sig_config_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 2. 统计/资源数据变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：脚本 OCR 读到了新体力 -> UI 进度条刷新
    sig_stats_updated = Signal(str, object)

    # 3. 运行时状态变更信号: (key, new_value)
    # 场景：ADB 断开连接 -> UI 状态灯变红
    sig_runtime_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 4. 日志信号: (level, message)
    # 场景：把后台的 print/log 转发到 UI 的文本框里
    sig_log_msg = Signal(str, str)

    def __init__(self, instance_id, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        
        # --- 路径配置 ---
        # 你的结构: data/deep_sl_002/
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_root, instance_id)
        self.config_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "config.json")
        self.stats_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "statistics.json")

        # --- 线程安全锁 ---
        # RLock (可重入锁) 允许同一个线程多次获得锁，防止死锁
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        # --- 内存数据容器 ---
        self._config = {}   # 对应 config.json
        self._stats = {}    # 对应 statistics.json
        self._runtime = {   # 纯内存状态 (关机即焚)
            "is_running": False,       # 调度器运行状态
            "connect_status": "OFFLINE", # ADB 连接状态
            "current_task": "Idle"     # 当前正在执行的任务名
        }

        # --- 初始化 ---
        self._ensure_dirs()
        self.load_all()

    def _ensure_dirs(self):
        """确保专属文件夹及子文件夹结构完整"""
        # 1. 创建实例主目录 (data/deep_sl_002)
        if not os.path.exists(self.base_dir):
            os.makedirs(self.base_dir, exist_ok=True)
            
        # 2. 创建日志目录 (data/deep_sl_002/log)
        log_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "log")
        if not os.path.exists(log_dir):
            os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 3. 创建截图目录 (data/deep_sl_002/snapshots)
        snap_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        if not os.path.exists(snap_dir):
            os.makedirs(snap_dir, exist_ok=True)

    def load_all(self):
        """加载所有持久化数据"""
        # 1. 加载配置 (带默认值)
        default_config = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_config.json")
        if default_config:
            # 动态设置 instance_name 默认值
            default_config["instance_name"] = self.instance_id
        else:
            # Fallback if file missing
            default_config = {"instance_name": self.instance_id}
            
        self._load_json(self.config_path, self._config, default_config)

        # 2. 加载统计 (默认为空)
        default_stats = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_statistics.json")
        if not default_stats:
             default_stats = {}
             
        self._load_json(self.stats_path, self._stats, default_stats)

    def _load_default_json(self, path):
        """从模板文件加载默认配置"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading default json {path}: {e}")
        return None

    def _load_json(self, path, container, default):
        """通用的安全读取逻辑 (带简单的二级补全)"""
        with self._lock:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        saved_data = json.load(f)
                        
                        # 1. 先把默认值深拷贝进来
                        # (这样能保证拥有最全的字段结构)
                        import copy
                        merged_data = copy.deepcopy(default)
                        
                        # 2. 递归更新 (这里只做两层简单的合并，够用了)
                        for key, val in saved_data.items():
                            if isinstance(val, dict) and key in merged_data and isinstance(merged_data[key], dict):
                                # 如果是字典（比如 modules），进去更新里面的字段
                                merged_data[key].update(val)
                            else:
                                # 如果是普通值，直接覆盖
                                merged_data[key] = val
                        
                        # 3. 更新到内存容器
                        container.clear()
                        container.update(merged_data)
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    self.push_log(f"文件 {os.path.basename(path)} 损坏/解析失败: {e}", "ERROR")
                    # 损坏了就用默认值
                    container.clear()
                    container.update(default)
                    self._save_json(path, container)
            else:
                # 文件不存在，初始化为默认值
                container.update(default)
                self._save_json(path, container)

    def _save_json(self, path, container):
        """通用的安全保存逻辑"""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 临时写入再重命名，防止写入一半断电导致文件损坏 (Atomic Write 思想)
                temp_path = path + ".tmp"
                with open(temp_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(container, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
                
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    os.remove(path)
                os.rename(temp_path, path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.push_log(f"保存 {os.path.basename(path)} 失败: {e}", "ERROR")

    def save_config(self):
        """手动触发保存配置"""
        self._save_json(self.config_path, self._config)

    def save_stats(self):
        """手动触发保存统计"""
        self._save_json(self.stats_path, self._stats)

    # ==================================================
    # 🔧 数据操作接口 (Getters & Setters)
    # ==================================================

    # --- 1. 用户配置 (Config) ---
    
    def get_cfg(self, key_path, default=None):
        """
        读取配置
        用法: get_cfg("modules.hunting.enable")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, default)

    def set_cfg(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改配置 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_cfg("modules.hunting.enable", True)
        """
        # 如果值确实改变了
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, value):
            self.save_config() # 持久化
            self.sig_config_changed.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 2. 游戏数据 (Statistics) ---
    
    def get_stat(self, key_path, default=0):
        """
        读取游戏统计
        用法: get_stat("resources.wood")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, default)

    def set_stat(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改游戏数据 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_stat("stamina", 80)
        """
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, value):
            self.save_stats() # 持久化
            self.sig_stats_updated.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 3. 运行时状态 (Runtime Memory) ---

    def get_runtime(self, key, default=None):
        """读取内存状态"""
        with self._lock:
            return self._runtime.get(key, default)

    def set_runtime(self, key, value):
        """
        修改运行时状态 -> 仅内存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_runtime("connect_status", "ONLINE")
        """
        with self._lock:
            if self._runtime.get(key) != value:
                self._runtime[key] = value
                self.sig_runtime_changed.emit(key, value) # 通知 UI

    # ==================================================
    # 🔨 辅助工具 (Helpers)
    # ==================================================

    def push_log(self, message, level="INFO"):
        """
        发送日志信号 (给 UI 显示用)
        并不负责写 log 文件 (写文件由 logging 模块在别处处理)
        """
        self.sig_log_msg.emit(level, message)

    def _get_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, default):
        """
        [内部工具] 解析点号路径: 'a.b.c' -> dict['a']['b']['c']
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                for k in keys:
                    data = data[k]
                return data
            except (KeyError, TypeError):
                return default

    def _set_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, value):
        """
        [内部工具] 设置点号路径: 'a.b.c' = value
        返回 True 表示值发生了变化 (需要保存/刷新)
        返回 False 表示值没变 (节省 IO)
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 递归找到倒数第二层
                for k in keys[:-1]:
                    # 如果中间层不存在，自动创建空字典
                    data = data.setdefault(k, {})
                
                last_key = keys[-1]
                old_value = data.get(last_key)
                
                if old_value != value:
                    data[last_key] = value
                    return True # 变了
                return False # 没变
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"设置键值失败 {path}: {e}")
                return False

# 文件结束

# KeyAuth 资源保护实施计划 (Memory Loading - Simplified)

本计划旨在通过将关键识别图片移至 KeyAuth 云端，并采用**内存加载 (Memory Loading)** 技术，构建高安全性的“无文件落地”防御体系。

## 1. 核心策略
*   **资源依赖**: 将脚本运行必不可少的 4 张关键图片上传至 KeyAuth Files。
*   **内存加载**: 客户端启动后，从云端下载图片字节流 (bytes) 直接存入内存，**绝不写入硬盘**。
*   **极简模板**: 实现 `MemoryTemplate` 类，仅重写读取逻辑，移除不必要的坐标和分辨率参数。

## 2. 保护对象 (KeyAuth Files)

需在 KeyAuth 后台上传以下图片，并记录对应的 File ID：

| 原始路径 | 资源 Key (代码引用名) | 重要性 | 作用 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `assets/images/home/home_check.png` | `img_home_check` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 脚本启动基石，判断是否在主页 |
| `assets/images/combat/formation1.png` | `img_formation` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 战斗任务入口判断 |
| `assets/images/combat/add.png` | `img_add` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 选将逻辑核心 |
| `assets/images/combat/clear_full.png` | `img_clear_full` | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 兵力设置逻辑 |

## 3. 代码实施步骤

### 步骤 1: 创建 `config/resource_loader.py`
实现 `ResourceLoader` 单例类，负责：
*   **存储**: 维护一个 `_memory_cache` 字典 (key -> bytes)。
*   **下载**: `load(keyauth_app, file_id, resource_key)` 方法，将云端文件流存入字典。
*   **获取**: `get_bytes(resource_key)` 方法，返回图片的二进制数据。

### 步骤 2: 创建 `config/memory_template.py`
实现 `MemoryTemplate` 类，继承自 `airtest.core.api.Template`：
*   **构造函数**: 仅接收 `image_bytes` 和 `threshold` 等基础参数。**不强制设置 resolution 和 record_pos**。
*   **重写 `_imread`**: 使用 `cv2.imdecode` 将内存中的 `bytes` 解码为 OpenCV 图像对象，替代原有的文件读取逻辑。

### 步骤 3: 修改 `KSAS.py` (启动加载)
在登录成功后的逻辑块中：
1.  实例化 `ResourceLoader`。
2.  调用 `load()` 方法依次下载上述 4 张图片到内存。
    *   *注意*: 需处理下载失败的情况，确保核心资源就位。

### 步骤 4: 修改任务代码 (使用 MemoryTemplate)
修改 `tasks/combat/combat_task.py` 等文件：

**示例 (CombatTask):**
```python
# 原代码
IMG_FORMATION = Template(r"assets/images/combat/formation1.png", threshold=0.9)

# 新代码
from config.resource_loader import ResourceLoader
from config.memory_template import MemoryTemplate

class CombatTask(BaseTask):
    def __init__(self, ...):
        # 从内存获取数据
        img_bytes = ResourceLoader().get_bytes("img_formation")
        
        if not img_bytes: 
            raise Exception("Resource missing: formation1")
            
        # 使用 MemoryTemplate (极简调用)
        self.IMG_FORMATION = MemoryTemplate(img_bytes, threshold=0.9)
```

## 4. 调试与发布
*   **Debug 模式**: 在 `ResourceLoader` 中增加 `DEBUG` 开关。开启时，读取本地 `assets/` 文件并转为 bytes 返回，方便开发调试。
*   **发布模式**: 关闭 `DEBUG` 开关，强制从 KeyAuth 下载。

FILE: .\instance_manager.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# instance_manager.py
import os
import json
import logging
import re
import time
import shutil
from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

from datastore import InstanceDataStore
from scheduler import InstanceScheduler
from task_registry import get_all_tasks
from basic import DeviceOperator
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class InstanceManager(QObject):
    """
    实例管理器
    """
    sig_instance_list_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.data_root = data_root
        self.registry_path = os.path.join(data_root, "device_registry.json")
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("InstanceManager")

        self.data_stores = {} 
        self.schedulers = {} 
        self._registry = {}

        self._load_registry()
        self._init_all_instances()

    def _load_registry(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.registry_path):
            try:
                with open(self.registry_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    content = f.read().strip()
                    if content:
                        self._registry = json.loads(content)
                    else:
                        self._registry = {}
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_registry_corrupted", e))
                self._registry = {}
        else:
            self._registry = {}

    def _save_registry(self):
        try:
            with open(self.registry_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self._registry, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_save_registry_failed", e))

    def _init_all_instances(self):
        """启动时加载所有已注册账号"""
        task_classes = get_all_tasks()

        for instance_id, info in self._registry.items():
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_loading_instance", info.get('display_name'), instance_id))
            store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = store
            
            try:
                device = DeviceOperator(info['serial'], instance_id)
                # device.connect() # 启动时不强制连接，为了速度
                sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, store, task_classes)
                
                # 🔄 【绑定】设置 DeviceOperator 的中断回调
                # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理，避免闭包引用问题)
                # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
                
                self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched
                
                # 🧹 启动自动清理 (异步或同步均可，这里用同步简单处理，因为文件IO通常很快)
                self._cleanup_files(instance_id)
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_load_scheduler_failed", instance_id, e))

    def create_instance(self, display_name, serial, diana_level=0, march_slots=5):
        safe_name = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '_', display_name).lower()
        instance_id = safe_name
        counter = 1
        while instance_id in self._registry:
            instance_id = f"{safe_name}_{counter}"
            counter += 1

        try:
            self._registry[instance_id] = {
                "display_name": display_name,
                "serial": serial,
                "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            }
            self._save_registry()

            new_store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.serial", serial)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.alias", display_name)
            # 设置 Diana 等级和行军队列
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(diana_level))
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(march_slots))
            
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = new_store

            device = DeviceOperator(serial, instance_id)
            device.connect()
            
            task_classes = get_all_tasks()
            sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, new_store, task_classes)
            
            # 🔄 【绑定】设置中断回调
            # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理)
            # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
            
            self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched

            self.logger.info(get_text("log_instance_created", display_name))
            self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
            return True, instance_id

        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_create_instance_failed", e))
            if instance_id in self._registry:
                del self._registry[instance_id]
            return False, str(e)

    def delete_instance(self, instance_id):
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_deleting_instance", instance_id))

        if instance_id in self.schedulers:
            try:
                sched = self.schedulers[instance_id]
                if sched.isRunning():
                    sched.stop_scheduler()
                time.sleep(0.1)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_stop_scheduler_error", e))
            del self.schedulers[instance_id]

        if instance_id in self.data_stores:
            del self.data_stores[instance_id]

        dir_path = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
        if os.path.exists(dir_path):
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(dir_path)
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_folder_deleted", dir_path))
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_delete_failed", e))

        del self._registry[instance_id]
        self._save_registry()
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()

    def update_instance(self, instance_id, new_serial=None, new_alias=None, new_diana_level=None, new_march_slots=None):
        """
        更新实例信息
        """
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return False

        # 1. 更新注册表 (只存核心信息)
        if new_serial:
            self._registry[instance_id]['serial'] = new_serial
        if new_alias:
            self._registry[instance_id]['display_name'] = new_alias
        self._save_registry()

        # 2. 更新 DataStore (存配置)
        store = self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
        if store:
            if new_serial:
                store.set_cfg("meta.serial", new_serial)
                if instance_id in self.schedulers:
                    self.schedulers[instance_id].device.serial = new_serial
            if new_alias:
                store.set_cfg("meta.alias", new_alias)
            
            # 👇 【新增】保存 Diana 等级
            if new_diana_level is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(new_diana_level))

            # 👇 【新增】保存行军队列数量
            if new_march_slots is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(new_march_slots))
        
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
        return True

    def get_all_instances(self):
        result = []
        for iid, info in self._registry.items():
            store = self.data_stores.get(iid)
            status = store.get_runtime("connect_status", "OFFLINE") if store else "ERROR"
            result.append({
                "id": iid,
                "name": info["display_name"],
                "serial": info["serial"],
                "status": status
            })
        return result

    def get_store(self, instance_id):
        return self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
    
    def get_scheduler(self, instance_id):
        return self.schedulers.get(instance_id)

    def _cleanup_files(self, instance_id):
        """清理过期日志和截图 (保留7天)"""
        try:
            base_dir = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
            targets = [
                os.path.join(base_dir, "log"),
                os.path.join(base_dir, "snapshots")
            ]
            
            now = time.time()
            retention_days = 7
            retention_sec = retention_days * 86400
            
            for folder in targets:
                if not os.path.exists(folder):
                    continue
                    
                for filename in os.listdir(folder):
                    file_path = os.path.join(folder, filename)
                    # 仅处理文件
                    if os.path.isfile(file_path):
                        # 获取修改时间
                        mtime = os.path.getmtime(file_path)
                        if now - mtime > retention_sec:
                            try:
                                os.remove(file_path)
                                self.logger.info(get_text("log_cleanup_file", instance_id, filename))
                            except Exception as del_err:
                                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_cleanup_file_failed", filename, del_err))
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_cleanup_error", instance_id, e))

os.environ["OPENCV_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"

# 引入 KeyAuth
from tools.keyauth.keyauth import api
from ui.login_dialog import LoginDialog
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader

def getchecksum():
    # 简单的 checksum 占位符，实际应读取文件 MD5
    return "checksum_placeholder"

def init_keyauth():
    return api(
        name = "KSAS",
        ownerid = "NVEZ7gY47U",
        version = "1.0",
        hash_to_check = getchecksum()
    )

def setup_global_adb():
    """
    设置 Airtest 全局 ADB 路径 (绝对路径 + 和平共处版)
    """
    print("-" * 30)
    print("[System] 正在初始化 ADB 设置...")
    
    use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
    custom_path = global_config.get("adb_path", "")
    
    target_adb = None
    
    # 1. 【核心修改】获取 KSAS.py 文件所在的绝对目录
    # 这确保了无论脚本在哪里运行，都能找到旁边的 tools 文件夹
    project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    
    if use_internal:
        # 拼接绝对路径
        internal_path = os.path.join(project_root, "tools", "adb", "adb.exe")
        if os.path.exists(internal_path):
            target_adb = internal_path
        else:
            print(f"❌ [System] 内置 ADB 文件未找到: {internal_path}")
            print("   (请确保 tools/adb/adb.exe 存在且结构正确)")
    elif custom_path and os.path.exists(custom_path):
        target_adb = custom_path
            
    if target_adb:
        adb_dir = os.path.dirname(target_adb)
        print(f"✅ [System] 锁定目标 ADB: {target_adb}")
        
        # 2. 【核心】修改 Airtest 内部常量
        constant.ADB_PATH = target_adb
        
        # 3. 【核心】将我们的 ADB 目录插入系统 PATH 的第一位
        # 这能拦截大部分直接调用 "adb" 命令的库
        os.environ["PATH"] = adb_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

        # 4. 【核心】修正 adbutils 库的指向 (如果有的话)
        # 这就是解决你刚才看到“指向 site-packages”问题的关键
        try:
            import adbutils
            adbutils._adb_path = target_adb
            print("[System] 已修正 adbutils 库的路径指向")
        except ImportError:
            pass 
            
    else:
        print("⚠️ [System] 未找到指定 ADB，将使用系统默认路径 (可能会导致兼容性问题)")
    
    print("-" * 30)

# 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(

    format='%(asctime)s - [%(name)s] - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
)
logger = logging.getLogger("Main")
