# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import time
import logging
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from PySide6.QtCore import QThread, Signal

# 引入日志桥梁
from logging_setup import GuiLogHandler, setup_file_logging
# 引入异常
from basic import TaskInterruptedError

class InstanceScheduler(QThread):
    """
    ALAS 风格的智能调度器 (优化版)
    
    特性：
    1. 任务调度：原子执行 + 循环扫描。
    2. 体力回复：独立倒计时逻辑 (5分钟回1点)，不受任务打断。
    """
    
    def __init__(self, instance_id, device, data, task_classes):
        super().__init__()
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{instance_id}.Scheduler")
        
        # 1. 挂载日志桥梁 (UI显示)
        self.instance_root_logger = logging.getLogger(instance_id)
        self.instance_root_logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        self.gui_handler = GuiLogHandler(data)
        self.instance_root_logger.addHandler(self.gui_handler)
        
        # 2. 挂载文件日志 (文件存储)
        import os
        log_dir = os.path.join(data.base_dir, "log")
        self.file_handler = setup_file_logging(instance_id, log_dir)

        self._is_running = False
        
        # 3. 智能闹钟 (Event)
        self._wake_event = threading.Event()

        # 4. ⏳ 独立体力计时器 (Next Stamina Tick)
        # 存储下一次回复体力的绝对时间 (datetime 对象)
        # 初始化为 None，表示闹钟目前是关着的
        self._next_stamina_tick = None
        
        # 5. 信号连接
        self.data.sig_config_changed.connect(self.on_config_changed)
        self.data.sig_stats_updated.connect(self.on_stat_changed)

        # 6. 装载任务实例
        self.tasks = []
        for TaskCls in task_classes:
            try:
                task_obj = TaskCls(device, data)
                self.tasks.append(task_obj)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"任务 {TaskCls.__name__} 初始化失败: {e}")

        # 7. 绑定中断回调 (直接绑定到当前实例的方法)
        self.device.interrupt_callback = self._check_interrupt

    def _check_interrupt(self):
        """
        检查是否应该中断设备操作
        返回 True 表示需要中断
        """
        # 如果调度器不在运行状态，则中断
        return not self._is_running

    # ==========================================
    # 📡 信号处理
    # ==========================================
    
    def on_config_changed(self, key, value):
        """配置变更：立即唤醒"""
        if self._is_running:
            self._wake_event.set()

    def on_stat_changed(self, key, value):
        """任务时间变更：立即唤醒"""
        if self._is_running and "next_run" in key:
            self._wake_event.set()

    # ==========================================
    # 🎮 控制接口
    # ==========================================
    
    @property
    def is_running_flag(self):
        """对外暴露的运行状态标志"""
        # self.logger.debug(f"Checking is_running_flag: {self._is_running}")
        return self._is_running

    def start_scheduler(self):
        if not self.isRunning():
            self._is_running = True
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", True)
            self._wake_event.clear()
            self.start()
            self.logger.info("调度器已启动")

    def stop_scheduler(self):
        if self.isRunning():
            self._is_running = False
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", False)
            self._wake_event.set() # 唤醒退出
            self.logger.info("正在停止调度器...")
            self.wait()
            
            self.instance_root_logger.removeHandler(self.gui_handler)
            self.logger.info("调度器已停止")

    # ==========================================
    # 🔄 核心循环 (大脑)
    # ==========================================

    def run(self):
        self.logger.info("调度器线程已启动")
        
        # 1. 飞行前安检
        if not self.device.connect():
            self.logger.error("❌ 无法连接设备，调度器自动停止。")
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", False)
            return

        # 🏠 启动时先尝试回到首页 (初始化状态)
        try:
            self.logger.info("正在初始化游戏状态 (回到首页)...")
            self.device.ensure_home()
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"初始化回城失败: {e} (将尝试继续运行)")

        while self._is_running:
            self._wake_event.clear()
            now = datetime.now()
            
            # ==========================================
            # 🟢 模块一：体力自动回复逻辑 (独立倒计时)
            # ==========================================
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            
            if current_stamina >= 200:
                # 满体力：关闭闹钟
                self._next_stamina_tick = None
            else:
                # 不满体力：检查闹钟
                if self._next_stamina_tick is None:
                    # 如果闹钟没开，现在立刻开启 (当前时间 + 300秒)
                    self._next_stamina_tick = now + timedelta(seconds=300)
                    # self.logger.debug(f"体力不满，开始回复计时 (下次: {self._next_stamina_tick.strftime('%H:%M:%S')})")
                
                # 检查时间是否到了
                if now >= self._next_stamina_tick:
                    # 时间到！体力+1
                    new_val = current_stamina + 1
                    self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_val)
                    self.logger.info(f"💧 体力自然回复 +1 (当前: {new_val})")
                    
                    # 闹钟往后顺延 300秒 (保持节奏)
                    self._next_stamina_tick += timedelta(seconds=300)
                    
                    # 防御性编程：如果休眠太久(比如关机了)，tick 时间远远落后于 now，就重置为 now+300
                    if self._next_stamina_tick < now:
                         self._next_stamina_tick = now + timedelta(seconds=300)

            # ==========================================
            # 🟡 模块二：任务扫描逻辑
            # ==========================================
            ready_tasks = []
            next_task_time = None 

            for task in self.tasks:
                if not task.enabled:
                    continue 
                
                if now >= task.next_run:
                    ready_tasks.append(task)
                else:
                    # 记录最近的一个未来任务时间
                    if next_task_time is None or task.next_run < next_task_time:
                        next_task_time = task.next_run

            # ==========================================
            # 🔵 模块三：决策与执行
            # ==========================================
            if ready_tasks:
                # A. 有任务：按优先级执行
                ready_tasks.sort(key=lambda t: t.priority)
                winner = ready_tasks[0]
                
                self.data.set_runtime("current_task", winner.name)
                
                try:
                    winner.execute()
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    self.logger.warning(f"🛑 任务 {winner.name} 被用户强制中断！")
                    self.data.set_runtime("current_task", "Interrupted")
                    
                    # 🏠 中断后尝试回到首页 (善后处理)
                    # 必须先临时禁用中断回调，否则 ensure_home 里的点击操作也会被立刻中断
                    original_callback = self.device.interrupt_callback
                    self.device.interrupt_callback = None
                    
                    try:
                        self.logger.info("正在尝试回到首页...")
                        self.device.ensure_home()
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.logger.error(f"回城失败: {e}")
                    finally:
                        # 恢复回调 (虽然线程马上要退出了，但保持状态一致性是个好习惯)
                        self.device.interrupt_callback = original_callback

                    # 中断后不需要 continue，直接让 while 循环去判断 self._is_running 即可
                
                # 跑完立即进入下一次循环 (不 sleep)，以便快速响应
                continue
            
            else:
                # B. 没任务：计算休眠时间
                # 我们需要同时考虑 "下个任务的时间" 和 "下个体力的时间"
                # 谁先到，就睡到谁那个时候醒
                
                wait_seconds = 3600 # 默认睡很久
                status_msg = "无任务，待机中..."

                # 1. 考虑任务
                if next_task_time:
                    delta_task = (next_task_time - now).total_seconds()
                    wait_seconds = min(wait_seconds, delta_task)
                    status_msg = f"休眠 (任务 {int(delta_task)}s 后)"

                # 2. 考虑体力 (为了准时加体力，不能睡过头)
                if self._next_stamina_tick:
                    delta_stamina = (self._next_stamina_tick - now).total_seconds()
                    # 至少睡 0.1 秒防止 CPU 空转
                    delta_stamina = max(0.1, delta_stamina)
                    wait_seconds = min(wait_seconds, delta_stamina)
                    # 如果体力马上就要回了，状态栏也可以显示一下
                    if delta_stamina < wait_seconds: 
                        status_msg = f"休眠 (回体 {int(delta_stamina)}s 后)"

                # 确保至少睡一点点，防止逻辑死循环跑太快
                wait_seconds = max(0.5, wait_seconds)
                
                self.data.set_runtime("current_task", status_msg)
                
                # 智能休眠
                self._wake_event.wait(wait_seconds)

# tasks/task_registry.py

# 1. 引入你所有的任务类
from tasks.example_task import ExampleTask
from tasks.combat.beast import BeastTask
from tasks.combat.wolf import WolfTask
from tasks.combat.rally import RallyTask
from tasks.daily.intel import IntelTask
from tasks.daily.gather import GatherTask
from tasks.shop.general_shop import ShopTask
from tasks.test_sidebar import TestSidebarTask
from tasks.daily.alliance import AllianceTechTask, AllianceChestTask

# 2. 把它们放进一个列表
TASK_CLASSES = [
    TestSidebarTask, # 测试任务放最前面方便找
    # ExampleTask, # 示例任务可以先注释掉
    BeastTask,
    WolfTask,
    RallyTask,
    IntelTask,
    GatherTask,
    ShopTask,
    AllianceTechTask,
    AllianceChestTask,
]

def get_all_tasks():
    return TASK_CLASSES

FILE: .\assets\images\wolf\新建文本文档.txt
==================================================
狼的逻辑：

前提判断：

1. 用户是否有行军队列。

2. 检查用户是否使用戴安娜，使用则调取用户戴安娜技能等级下的对应rally体力消耗，和用户现有体力进行对比。如果体力足够则进行下面的运行逻辑，体力不够则推迟任务，推迟时间按照缺少的体力*5分钟。





1. ensure home

2. 点击backpack按钮，

3. 点击指定坐标

4. 扫描wolf文件夹内的check.png是否存在

是：检测wolf.png是否存在，存在则点击，不存在则返回说没有狼了。

否：从ensure home重新开始，最多三次。

FILE: .\game\hero_manager.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

class HeroManager:
    """
    英雄状态管理器
    负责管理英雄的拥有状态、出击队伍信息，并与 DataStore 进行同步。
    """

    # 英雄白名单
    # 格式: 内部代号 (小写)
    HERO_LIST = [
        # Gen 4
        "rosa", "alcar", "margot",
        # Gen 3
        "jaeger", "eric", "petra",
        # Gen 2
        "hilde", "zoe", "marlin",
        # Gen 1
        "jabel", "amadeus", "helga", "saul",
        # SR (Epic)
        "amane", "yeonwoo", "fahd", "chenko", "gordon", "diana", "howard", "quinn",
        # R (Rare)
        "olive", "edwin", "seth", "forrest",
    ]

    def __init__(self, data):
        """
        :param data: InstanceDataStore 实例
        """
        self.data = data

    def update_hero_status(self, hero_name, owned=None, state=None):
        """
        更新英雄状态
        :param hero_name: 英雄代号 (必须在 HERO_LIST 中)
        :param owned: bool, 是否拥有 (None 表示不更新此项) -> 存入 config
        :param state: str, 状态 "idle" / "busy" (None 表示不更新此项) -> 存入 stats
        """
        if hero_name not in self.HERO_LIST:
            return

        # 1. 更新拥有状态 (Config)
        if owned is not None:
            self.data.set_cfg(f"heroes.{hero_name}", owned)
        
        # 2. 更新状态信息 (Stats)
        if state is not None:
            self.data.set_stat(f"heroes.{hero_name}", state)

    def get_hero_info(self, hero_name):
        """
        获取单个英雄的完整信息
        :return: dict { "owned": bool, "state": str }
        """
        # 拥有状态从 config 读取
        owned = self.data.get_cfg(f"heroes.{hero_name}", False)
        # 状态信息从 stats 读取
        state = self.data.get_stat(f"heroes.{hero_name}", "idle")
        
        return {"owned": owned, "state": state}

    def get_owned_heroes(self):
        """
        获取所有已拥有的英雄列表
        :return: list [hero_name, ...]
        """
        # 从 config 读取所有英雄配置
        all_heroes_cfg = self.data.get_cfg("heroes", {})
        owned_list = []
        for name, is_owned in all_heroes_cfg.items():
            if is_owned:
                owned_list.append(name)
        return owned_list

    def get_heroes_by_state(self, target_state="idle"):
        """
        获取指定状态的英雄列表
        :param target_state: "idle" or "busy"
        :return: list [hero_name, ...]
        """
        # 从 stats 读取所有英雄状态信息
        all_heroes_stats = self.data.get_stat("heroes", {})
        result = []
        for name, state in all_heroes_stats.items():
            if state == target_state:
                result.append(name)
        return result

    def is_hero_available(self, hero_name):
        """
        检查英雄是否可用 (已拥有且空闲)
        """
        info = self.get_hero_info(hero_name)
        # 拥有且状态为空闲
        return info.get("owned", False) and (info.get("state") == "idle")

==================================================
FILE: .\game\sidebar.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import logging
import cv2
import os
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class Sidebar:
    """
    游戏侧边栏交互类
    负责处理侧边栏的展开/收起，以及读取侧边栏上的信息（如行军队列状态）
    """
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("Game.Sidebar")
        
        # 初始化空闲队列数量 (从统计数据中读取，默认为 0)
        self.idle_march_count = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0)

        # --- 资源定义 ---
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_SIDEBAR_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/sidebar_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_SIDEBAR_QUEUE_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/sidebar_queue_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # --- 坐标与区域定义 ---
        self.POS_EXPAND_BTN = (4, 550)
        self.AREA_CHECK = (6, 242, 444, 297)
        
        # 侧边栏页面切换按钮
        self.POS_TOWN_PAGE_BTN = (170, 270)
        self.POS_MARCH_PAGE_BTN = (290, 270)

        # 行军队列检测区域 (x1, y1, x2, y2)
        self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS = [
            (370, 340, 430, 400), # Queue 1
            (370, 415, 430, 475), # Queue 2
            (370, 490, 430, 550), # Queue 3
            (370, 560, 430, 625), # Queue 4
            (370, 637, 430, 696), # Queue 5
            (370, 710, 430, 770)  # Queue 6
        ]

    def expand(self):
        """
        展开侧边栏
        逻辑:
        1. 检查是否有 btn_town.png (判断是否在野外)
           - 是: 点击 (4, 550)
           - 否: 点击 btn_go_map.png -> 等待 1.5s -> 点击 (4, 550)
        2. 检查 sidebar_check.png 是否出现
        """
        self.logger.info("Expanding sidebar...")
        
        

        for i in range(3):
            self.logger.info(f"Expand attempt {i+1}/3")
            # 0. 确保在主界面
            self.device.ensure_home()

            
            # 1. 场景判断与点击
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_wilderness"))
                self.device.click(self.POS_EXPAND_BTN)
            else:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_switch_map"))
                if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                    self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                    self.device.sleep(1.5)
                    self.device.click(self.POS_EXPAND_BTN)
                else:
                    self.logger.warning("Neither btn_town nor btn_go_map found, skipping click.")

            # 2. 结果验证
            self.device.sleep(1.0) # 等待动画
            
            # 使用区域截图进行验证
            # AREA_CHECK = (6, 242, 444, 297) -> (x1, y1, x2, y2)
            screen_path = self.device._save_snapshot("sidebar_check")
            if screen_path:
                screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
                if screen is not None:
                    # Crop: y1:y2, x1:x2
                    x1, y1, x2, y2 = self.AREA_CHECK
                    cropped = screen[y1:y2, x1:x2]
                    
                    # 匹配
                    res = self.IMG_SIDEBAR_CHECK.match_in(cropped)
                    if res:
                        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_success"))
                        return True
        
        self.logger.error(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_failed"))
        return False

    def collapse(self):
        """
        收起侧边栏
        逻辑: 点击 btn_town.png -> 等待 1.5s -> 点击 btn_go_map.png
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_collapsing"))
        
        # 1. 点击回城
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN)
        
        # 2. 等待
        self.device.sleep(1.5)
        
        # 3. 点击出城
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
            self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)

    def get_idle_march_queues(self):
        """
        获取空闲行军队列数量
        逻辑:
        1. 展开侧边栏
        2. 点击行军页面按钮
        3. 循环检测 sidebar_queue_check.png
        4. 计算空闲数量并保存
        :return: int 空闲队列数量
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_checking_queues"))

        # 1. 展开侧边栏
        if not self.expand():
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_failed"))
            return 0

        # 2. 点击行军页面按钮
        self.device.click(self.POS_MARCH_PAGE_BTN)
        self.device.sleep(0.4) # 等待切换

        # 3. 获取用户设置的总队列数
        total_slots = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", 5)
        
        # 4. 循环检测
        # 假设: 队列从上到下排列，如果上面的忙碌，下面的才可能是空闲？
        # 或者: 只要有一个空闲，它下面的所有队列也都是空闲的？(通常游戏逻辑如此)
        # 逻辑: 找到第一个"没有 sidebar_queue_check.png" (即空闲) 的位置
        
        first_idle_index = -1
        
        # 截取屏幕一次，提高效率 (假设所有队列都在一屏内)
        screen_path = self.device._save_snapshot("march_check")
        if not screen_path:
            return 0
        screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
        if screen is None:
            return 0

        for i in range(total_slots):
            if i >= len(self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS):
                break
                
            area = self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS[i]
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = area
            
            # 裁剪区域
            cropped = screen[y1:y2, x1:x2]
            
            # 检测忙碌图标 (sidebar_queue_check.png)
            # 如果存在 -> 忙碌
            # 如果不存在 -> 空闲
            if not self.IMG_SIDEBAR_QUEUE_CHECK.match_in(cropped):
                first_idle_index = i
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_queue_idle_found", i + 1))
                break
        
        # 5. 计算结果
        if first_idle_index != -1:
            # 如果在索引 i 处发现空闲，说明从 i 开始到底部都是空闲的
            # 例如: 总数 5，索引 2 (第3个) 空闲 -> 空闲数为 5 - 2 = 3 (即 3, 4, 5)
            idle_count = total_slots - first_idle_index
        else:
            # 循环结束都没 break，说明全部忙碌
            idle_count = 0
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_idle_count", idle_count))
        
        # 6. 保存并更新
        self.idle_march_count = idle_count
        self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", idle_count)
        
        return idle_count

==================================================
FILE: .\i18n\i18n.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import yaml
import logging
from config import global_config

logger = logging.getLogger("i18n")

# ==========================================
# 📖 翻译缓存 (Translation Cache)
# ==========================================
# 结构: { "zh_CN": {key: value}, "en_US": {key: value} }
_TRANSLATION_CACHE = {}

def load_language(lang_code):
    """
    加载指定语言的 YAML 文件
    :param lang_code: e.g. "zh", "en"
    """
    # 映射: zh_CN -> zh, en_US -> en
    short_lang = "zh" if "zh" in lang_code else "en"
    
    if short_lang in _TRANSLATION_CACHE:
        return _TRANSLATION_CACHE[short_lang]

    # 获取当前文件所在目录
    base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    file_path = os.path.join(base_dir, f"{short_lang}.yaml")
    
    data = {}
    if os.path.exists(file_path):
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
            logger.info(f"已加载语言包: {short_lang}.yaml")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"加载语言包失败 {file_path}: {e}")
    else:
        logger.warning(f"语言包不存在: {file_path}")
        
    _TRANSLATION_CACHE[short_lang] = data
    return data

# 预加载默认语言
load_language("zh")
load_language("en")

# ==========================================
# 🔧 工具函数 (Helper Functions)
# ==========================================

def get_text(key, *args):
    """
    获取翻译文本
    :param key: 字典中的键
    :param args: 如果文本中有 {} 占位符，用这些参数填充
    Usage: get_text("msg_del_warn", "InstanceA")
    """
    # 1. 从全局配置读取语言
    current_lang_code = global_config.get("language", "zh_CN")
    short_lang = "zh" if "zh" in current_lang_code else "en"
    
    # 2. 获取对应语言的字典
    lang_data = _TRANSLATION_CACHE.get(short_lang)
    if not lang_data:
        # 尝试重新加载
        lang_data = load_language(short_lang)
    
    # 3. 查找 Key
    text = lang_data.get(key)
    
    # 4. 如果没找到，尝试回退到中文 (Fallback)
    if text is None and short_lang != "zh":
        fallback_data = _TRANSLATION_CACHE.get("zh")
        if fallback_data:
            text = fallback_data.get(key)
            
    # 5. 还是没找到，直接返回 Key
    if text is None:
        return key
    
    # 6. 格式化参数
    if args:
        try:
            # 确保 text 是字符串
            return str(text).format(*args)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"格式化翻译文本失败 [{key}]: {e}")
            return str(text)
            
    return str(text)

# tasks/base_task.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

import os
import logging
import time
from airtest.core.api import Template
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from game.sidebar import Sidebar

class BaseTask:
    """
    所有任务的基类 (ALAS 风格)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{self.data.instance_id}.Task.{self.name}")
        self.sidebar = Sidebar(device, data)

    # ==========================================
    # ⚙️ 属性配置
    # ==========================================
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "BaseTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 50

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 3600

    # ==========================================
    # ⏱️ 调度逻辑
    # ==========================================
    @property
    def enabled(self):
        return self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.{self.name.lower()}.enable", False)

    @property
    def next_run(self):
        time_str = self.data.get_stat(f"tasks.{self.name}.next_run", "1970-01-01 00:00:00")
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(time_str, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        except ValueError:
            return datetime.min 

    def set_next_run(self, seconds=None, timestamp=None):
        if timestamp:
            future_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp)
        else:
            if seconds is None:
                seconds = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.{self.name.lower()}.interval", self.default_interval)
            future_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(seconds=int(seconds))
            
        time_str = future_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        
        self.data.set_stat(f"tasks.{self.name}.next_run", time_str)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_next_run_scheduled", time_str))

    # ==========================================
    # 🏃 运行入口
    # ==========================================
    def execute(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_started", self.name))
        self.data.set_runtime("current_task", self.name)
        
        try:
            success = self.run_impl()
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_task_crashed", e))
            success = False
        
        if success:
            # 如果任务执行成功，但被禁用了（例如 WolfTask 次数耗尽），就不再安排下次运行
            if self.enabled:
                self.set_next_run()
            else:
                self.logger.info(f"任务 {self.name} 已禁用，停止调度")
        else:
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_finished", self.name))

    def run_impl(self):
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ==========================================
    # 🎮 通用游戏操作 (核心逻辑)
    # ==========================================

    def select_search_level(self, target_level):
        """
        通用的等级调整逻辑 (侦察 -> 归零 -> 调整 -> 核实)
        :param target_level: int, 目标等级 (1-29)
        """
        # ==========================================
        # 🛑 请务必修改这些坐标！(720x1280分辨率下)
        # ==========================================
        SLIDER_LEFT = (120, 1053) # 滑动条最左端 (能点到1级的位置)
        BTN_MINUS = (69, 1053)    # 减号按钮中心
        BTN_PLUS = (487, 1053)    # 加号按钮中心
        BTN_SEARCH = (360, 1214) # 底部巨大的搜索按钮
        
        # 图片路径模板
        IMG_LEVEL_TPL = r"assets/images/map_level/{}.png"
        IMG_MINUS_GRAY = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/grey_minus.png", threshold=0.95)

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_adjust_level_start", target_level))

        # --- 1. 侦察阶段 (Pre-Check) ---
        # 如果当前屏幕上已经显示了目标等级，直接去搜索，省时省力
        target_img_path = IMG_LEVEL_TPL.format(target_level)
        
        # 检查图片是否存在 (利用 basic.py 的 _exists_internal)
        if os.path.exists(target_img_path):
            target_tpl = Template(target_img_path, threshold=0.9)
            if self.device.exists(target_tpl):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_already_match", target_level))
                self.device.click(BTN_SEARCH)
                return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_level_img_missing", target_img_path))

        # --- 2. 归零阶段 (Reset to 1) ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_resetting"))
        
        # A. 猛击滑动条左侧 (快速降级)
        self.device.click(SLIDER_LEFT)
        self.device.sleep(0.5) # 等动画

        # B. 灰色检测与补刀 (确保降到 1)
        is_reset_success = False
        for _ in range(15): # 最多补点15次，防死循环
            if self.device.exists(IMG_MINUS_GRAY):
                # self.logger.debug("减号已变灰，归零成功")
                is_reset_success = True
                break
            else:
                # 还没灰，说明还没到1级，点减号补刀
                self.device.click(BTN_MINUS, times=1)
                # time.sleep(0.1) # click 内部已有 sleep，这里不用加太久

        if not is_reset_success:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_reset_warning"))

        # --- 3. 调整阶段 (Adjust) ---
        # 现在默认是 Lv.1
        clicks_needed = target_level - 1
        
        if clicks_needed > 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_upgrading", clicks_needed))
            # 为了速度，我们可以直接调用 adb 连点，绕过 device.click 的 sleep
            for _ in range(clicks_needed):
                self.device.adb.touch(BTN_PLUS)
                time.sleep(0.1) # 80ms 间隔，极速连点
            self.device.sleep(0.8) # 等数字跳完

        # --- 4. 核实阶段 (Verify) ---
        # 再看一眼是不是我们要的等级
        if os.path.exists(target_img_path):
            if not self.device.exists(target_tpl):
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_level_adjust_failed", target_level))
                # 失败策略：是重试还是硬着头皮搜？
                # 这里选择硬着头皮搜，万一只是图片识别不到呢
            else:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_verify_success", target_level))

        # --- 5. 搜索 ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_search"))
        self.device.click(BTN_SEARCH)
        self.device.sleep(1.5) # 等待界面切换
        return True

    # ==========================================
    # 📊 辅助功能
    # ==========================================

import time
from tasks.base_task import BaseTask

class ExampleTask(BaseTask):

    def name(self):
        return "ExampleTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 10 # 优先级

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 60 # 默认每 60 秒跑一次

    def run_impl(self):
        """
        模拟干活
        """
        self.logger.info(">>> ExampleTask 开始执行！正在模拟点击...")
        time.sleep(2) # 假装在点屏幕
        self.logger.info(">>> 点击完成，正在识别...")
        time.sleep(1) # 假装在 OCR
        self.logger.info(">>> 任务完成！")
        return True

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
import time

class TestSidebarTask(BaseTask):
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "TestSidebarTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 100  # 高优先级，方便测试

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info("=== 开始测试侧边栏功能 ===")
        
        # 1. 测试展开/收起 (get_idle_march_queues 内部会调用 expand)
        # self.sidebar.collapse() # 先收起一次，确保状态
        # time.sleep(1)

        # 2. 测试获取空闲队列
        self.logger.info("正在检测空闲行军队列...")
        idle_count = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        
        self.logger.info(f"检测结果: 空闲队列数量 = {idle_count}")
        
        # 3. 验证数据持久化
        saved_count = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", -1)
        if saved_count == idle_count:
            self.logger.info(f"数据持久化验证成功: statistics.json 中已保存为 {saved_count}")
        else:
            self.logger.error(f"数据持久化验证失败: 内存值 {idle_count} != 保存值 {saved_count}")

        self.logger.info("=== 测试结束 ===")
        return True

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text
import cv2

class CombatTask(BaseTask):
    """
    战斗任务基类
    处理通用的编队、选将、调兵逻辑
    """

    # 戴安娜减耗表 (技能等级: (集结体力, 打野体力))
    DIANA_COST_TABLE = {
        1: (23, 9),
        2: (22, 9),
        3: (22, 9),
        4: (21, 9),
        5: (20, 8)
    }
    DEFAULT_COST = (25, 10)

    # 战斗配置页面按钮
    IMG_CLEAR_FULL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/clear_full.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_EQUAL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/equal.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_ADD = Template(r"assets/images/combat/add.png", threshold=0.9)

    # Stamina Update Templates
    IMG_INTEL = Template(r"assets/images/home/intel.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        self.target_heroes = []
        self.soldier_count = 0
        
        # Number templates for stamina update
        self.number_templates = {}
        for i in range(10):
            self.number_templates[i] = Template(f"assets/images/number/{i}.png", threshold=0.9)

    def verify_combat_page(self):
        """
        确保在战斗配置页面
        """
        img_formation = Template(r"assets/images/combat/formation1.png", threshold=0.9)
        img_lock = Template(r"assets/images/combat/lock.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        if not (self.device.exists(img_formation) or self.device.exists(img_lock)):
            raise Exception(get_text("err_not_in_combat_page"))
        return True

    def calculate_cost(self, mode="rally"):
        skill_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        if skill_level == 0:
            return self.DEFAULT_COST[0] if mode == "rally" else self.DEFAULT_COST[1]

        if self.find_diana():
            costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(skill_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)
            cost = costs[0] if mode == "rally" else costs[1]
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_diana_cost", skill_level, cost))
            return cost
        else:
            cost = self.DEFAULT_COST[0] if mode == "rally" else self.DEFAULT_COST[1]
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_normal_cost", cost))
            return cost

    def find_diana(self):
        # 确保路径正确
        img_diana = Template(r"assets/images/heroes/diana_big.png", threshold=0.9)
        return self.device.exists(img_diana)

    def select_heroes(self, heroes: list):
        """
        根据传入的英雄列表进行选将
        :param heroes: list, 英雄名称列表 (e.g. ['diana', 'jessie'])
        """
        if not heroes:
            self.logger.info("未指定英雄，跳过选将")
            return

        # 0. 检测是否存在 add.png
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_ADD):
            self.logger.info("检测到已有英雄上阵，执行清除操作")
            # 1. 点击清除按钮 (237, 260)
            self.device.click((237, 260))
            self.device.sleep(2.5)
            
            # 1.1 再次确认 add.png
            # 注意：exists 方法内部会调用 _find_template_pos，它会重新截图
            if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_ADD):
                self.logger.warning("清除英雄失败，尝试继续...")
        
        # 2. 点击第一个英雄栏 (174, 374)
        self.logger.info("点击第一个英雄栏")
        self.device.click((174, 374))
        self.device.sleep(1) # 等待英雄选择界面加载

        # 3. 循环选择英雄
        for i, hero_name in enumerate(heroes):
            self.logger.info(f"正在寻找英雄: {hero_name} ({i+1}/{len(heroes)})")
            
            # 构建小头像 Template
            img_hero_small = Template(f"assets/images/heroes/{hero_name}_small.png", threshold=0.85)
            
            found = False
            # 尝试寻找 (最多拖动3次)
            for attempt in range(4): # 0 (初始) + 3 (拖动)
                if self.device.exists(img_hero_small):
                    self.logger.info(f"找到英雄: {hero_name}")
                    self.device.click(img_hero_small)
                    self.device.sleep(0.5)
                    found = True
                    break
                
                if attempt < 3:
                    self.logger.info(f"未找到 {hero_name}，尝试向下拖动 ({attempt+1}/3)")
                    # 起始点 (358, 905) -> 终止点 (358, 608)
                    self.device.swipe((358, 905), (358, 608), duration=1.5)
                    self.device.sleep(1) # 等待滚动停止
            
            if not found:
                raise Exception(f"无法找到目标英雄: {hero_name}")

            # 找到并点击后，点击确认按钮 (440, 1000)
            self.logger.info("点击确认按钮")
            self.device.click((440, 1000))
            self.device.sleep(0.5) # 等待跳转到下一个位置或返回
            
            # 注意：根据描述，按下确认后会自动跳转到下一个英雄选择页面
            # 所以不需要手动点击下一个栏位，除非是最后一个英雄选完后需要返回

        # 4. 英雄选择完毕后按一次 back 按键返回出击页面
        # 如果选完最后一个英雄，界面可能还在英雄选择页(如果还有空位)，或者自动返回了？
        # 根据描述 "英雄选择完毕后按一次back按键返回出击页面"，假设需要手动返回
        self.logger.info("英雄选择完毕，返回出击页面")
        self.device.keyevent("BACK")
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

    def set_one_soldier(self, enable=True):
        """
        设置是否以1兵出击
        :param enable: bool, True=1兵, False=平均分配
        """
        # 1兵选项坐标 (这个还是保留坐标，因为可能是弹窗里的选项)
        BTN_ONE_SOLDIER = (658, 690)
        
        if enable:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_set_one_soldier"))
            # 点击清除/满兵按钮 (打开设置菜单)
            self.device.click(self.IMG_CLEAR_FULL)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)
            # 点击1兵选项
            self.device.click(BTN_ONE_SOLDIER)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)
        else:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_set_avg_soldier"))
            # 点击平均分配按钮
            self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)

    def verify_attack_success(self):
        """
        验证出击是否成功
        判断屏幕上是否同时有：home_check.png 和 btn_town.png
        :return: bool
        """
        # img_home_check = Template(r"assets/images/home/home_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        img_home_check = self.device.IMG_HOME_CHECK
        img_btn_town = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # 等待一小段时间让界面切换
        self.device.sleep(1)
        
        if self.device.exists(img_home_check) and self.device.exists(img_btn_town):
            self.logger.info("出击成功 (检测到主界面特征)")
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning("出击失败 (未检测到主界面特征)")
            return False

    def check_stamina_sufficient(self, area=None):
        """
        检查体力是否足够
        逻辑：截取指定区域，检测是否存在红色像素
        :param area: (x1, y1, x2, y2) 默认为 (545, 1213, 620, 1245)
        :return: bool, True=足够(无红色), False=不足(有红色)
        """
        import cv2
        import numpy as np
        import os
        
        # 区域坐标 (x1, y1, x2, y2)
        if area is None:
            area = (545, 1213, 620, 1245)
        
        try:
            # snapshot() 返回临时文件路径
            screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
            
            # 如果 snapshot 返回的是 bytes (某些模式下)，需要处理
            if isinstance(screen_path, bytes):
                # 这里假设它返回的是路径。如果是 bytes，通常需要 decode 或者直接用 cv2.imdecode
                # 但 Airtest 的 snapshot 通常保存文件
                pass
            
            if not screen_path or not os.path.exists(screen_path):
                self.logger.error("截图失败或文件不存在")
                return False
            
            img = cv2.imread(screen_path)
            if img is None:
                self.logger.error("无法读取截图文件")
                return False
                
            # 裁剪区域 (y1:y2, x1:x2)
            # 注意 cv2 是 (height, width) 即 (y, x)
            # 确保坐标不越界
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = area
            x1, y1 = max(0, x1), max(0, y1)
            x2, y2 = min(w, x2), min(h, y2)
            
            crop_img = img[y1:y2, x1:x2]
            
            if crop_img.size == 0:
                self.logger.error("裁剪区域为空")
                return False

            # 转换为 HSV 颜色空间更容易检测红色
            hsv = cv2.cvtColor(crop_img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
            
            # 定义红色的范围
            # 红色在 HSV 中有两个区间：[0, 10] 和 [170, 180]
            lower_red1 = np.array([0, 70, 50])
            upper_red1 = np.array([10, 255, 255])
            lower_red2 = np.array([170, 70, 50])
            upper_red2 = np.array([180, 255, 255])
            
            mask1 = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower_red1, upper_red1)
            mask2 = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower_red2, upper_red2)
            mask = mask1 + mask2
            
            # 计算非零像素点数量
            red_pixels = cv2.countNonZero(mask)
            
            # 阈值设为 5 个像素，避免噪点
            if red_pixels > 5:
                self.logger.info(f"检测到红色像素 ({red_pixels})，体力不足")
                
                # 触发体力更新逻辑
                self.logger.info("触发体力更新流程...")
                
                # 1. 退出当前战斗窗口，返回主页
                self.device.ensure_home()
                
                # 2. 执行 update_stamina
                self.update_stamina()
                
                return False
            else:
                self.logger.info("未检测到红色像素，体力足够")
                return True
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"检查体力时出错: {e}")
            # 出错时保守起见返回 False (不足)，避免无限重试
            return False

    def detect_current_heroes(self):
        """
        检测当前页面显示的英雄
        :return: list, 英雄名称列表 (e.g. ['diana', 'jessie'])
        """
        import os
        
        detected_heroes = []
        heroes_dir = "assets/images/heroes"
        
        if not os.path.exists(heroes_dir):
            self.logger.warning(f"英雄图片目录不存在: {heroes_dir}")
            return []
            
        # 遍历目录下的所有文件
        for filename in os.listdir(heroes_dir):
            if filename.endswith("_big.png"):
                # 提取英雄名称 (去掉 _big.png)
                hero_name = filename.replace("_big.png", "")
                
                # 构建 Template
                img_path = os.path.join(heroes_dir, filename)
                tpl = Template(img_path, threshold=0.9)
                
                # 检测是否存在
                if self.device.exists(tpl):
                    self.logger.info(f"检测到英雄: {hero_name}")
                    detected_heroes.append(hero_name)
                    
        return detected_heroes

    def update_stamina(self):
        """
        执行体力更新逻辑
        """
        retry_count = 0
        max_retries = 1
        
        while retry_count <= max_retries:
            # Step 0: Ensure Home
            self.device.ensure_home()

            # Step 0.5: Check for btn_go_map
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                self.logger.info("Found btn_go_map, clicking...")
                self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            # Step 0_Click: Click Intel
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL):
                self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL)
            else:
                self.logger.warning("Intel icon not found on home screen")
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Wait 0.8s
            self.device.sleep(0.8)
            
            # Step 1: Snapshot and Crop
            # Area: 600, 25, 660, 53
            screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
            if not screen_path:
                self.logger.error("Snapshot failed")
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            img = cv2.imread(screen_path)
            if img is None:
                self.logger.error("Failed to read snapshot")
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Crop: y1:y2, x1:x2
            # Area from MD: 600, 25, 660, 53 (x1, y1, x2, y2)
            # OpenCV uses img[y:h, x:w]
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = 600, 25, 660, 53
            crop_img = img[y1:y2, x1:x2]
            
            # Step 2: Match Numbers
            results = [] # List of (x_coord, digit)
            
            # Loop 0-9
            for digit in range(10):
                template = self.number_templates[digit]
                # match_all_in returns list of results
                matches = template.match_all_in(crop_img)
                
                if matches:
                    for match in matches:
                        # match is usually a dict with 'result' (center point)
                        if isinstance(match, dict) and 'result' in match:
                            x = match['result'][0]
                            results.append((x, digit))
                        elif isinstance(match, (tuple, list)):
                                x = match[0]
                                results.append((x, digit))
                
                # Check Limit
                if len(results) >= 3:
                    break
            
            # Check Empty
            if not results:
                self.logger.warning(f"No numbers found in stamina area. Retry {retry_count}/{max_retries}")
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Sort by X coordinate
            results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            
            # Concat
            stamina_str = "".join([str(r[1]) for r in results])
            try:
                stamina_val = int(stamina_str)
            except ValueError:
                self.logger.error(f"Failed to parse stamina: {stamina_str}")
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Save
            self.logger.info(f"Updated Stamina: {stamina_val}")
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", stamina_val)
            
            # Return to home to be safe (optional, but good practice if we left it in Intel screen)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            
            # End
            return True
            
        self.logger.error("Failed to update stamina after retries")
        self.device.ensure_home()
        return False

from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from airtest.core.api import Template

class WolfTask(CombatTask):
    # UI 配置定义
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Wolf Task",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Combat"
        },
        {
            "key": "interval",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_interval",
            "min": 1,
            "max": 1440,
            "default": 450
        },
        {
            "key": "max_attacks",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_attacks",
            "min": 1,
            "max": 100,
            "default": 10
        },
        {
            "key": "one_soldier",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_one_soldier",
            "default": False
        },
        {
            "key": "heroes",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_hero",
            "options": [
                {"value": "none", "label": "opt_hero_any"},
                {"value": "diana", "label": "opt_hero_diana"},
                {"value": "diana_fahd", "label": "opt_hero_fahd_diana"}
            ],
            "default": "none"
        },
        {
            "key": "fallback_policy",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_fallback",
            "options": [
                {"value": "use_default", "label": "opt_fallback_default"},
                {"value": "wait_1h", "label": "opt_fallback_wait"}
            ],
            "default": "use_default"
        }
    ]
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "WolfTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 40

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        # 默认 7分30秒
        return 450

    def run_impl(self):
        # 获取配置
        # 注意：配置键名需要加上 modules.wolftask. 前缀
        use_one_soldier = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.one_soldier", False)
        hero_config = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.heroes", "none")
        fallback_policy = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.fallback_policy", "use_default")
        max_attacks = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.max_attacks", 10)
        
        # 检查次数限制
        # 注意：datastore.py 中定义的方法是 get_runtime(key, default)
        current_attacks = self.data.get_runtime("wolf.attack_count", 0)
        if current_attacks >= max_attacks:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_finished"))
            # 自动禁用任务
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.enable", False)
            return False

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_start", use_one_soldier, hero_config))

        # 0. 预检查 (Home Page, 空闲队列, 体力)
        if not self.pre_check(hero_config):
            return False

        # 1. 寻找 Wolf (Backpack -> Check -> Wolf -> Attack)
        if not self.find_and_click_wolf():
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_find_fail"))
            return False

        # 2. 战斗准备 (兵力 & 英雄)
        if not self.prepare_combat(use_one_soldier, hero_config, fallback_policy):
            return False

        # 3. 发起集结
        if self.start_rally():
            # 1. 更新出击计数 (减少剩余次数)
            # 注意：这里直接修改配置中的 max_attacks，从而同步到 UI
            new_max_attacks = max(0, max_attacks - 1)
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.max_attacks", new_max_attacks)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_attacks_left", new_max_attacks))
            
            # 如果剩余次数为0，自动禁用任务
            if new_max_attacks == 0:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_finished"))
                self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.enable", False)

            # 2. 扣除体力
            # 根据 Diana 技能等级计算消耗
            # 检查当前队列中是否有 Diana (使用父类 CombatTask 的 find_diana 方法)
            cost = 25 # 默认消耗
            if self.find_diana():
                # 获取 Diana 技能等级
                diana_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
                # 查表计算 (集结消耗)
                if diana_level > 0:
                    costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)
                    cost = costs[0] # 集结消耗是第一个值
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_diana_found", diana_level, cost))
            else:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_diana_not_found"))
            
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            new_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - cost)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_stamina)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_deduct_stamina", cost, new_stamina))
            
            # 3. 减少空闲队列
            current_idle = self.sidebar.idle_march_count
            new_idle = max(0, current_idle - 1)
            self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", new_idle)
            self.sidebar.idle_march_count = new_idle
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_deduct_queue", new_idle))
            
            return True
        
        return False

    def pre_check(self, hero_config):
        """
        0. 先确定在home page，确定有空闲队列，如果指定用戴安娜要确定体力>20，未指定英雄则确定体力>25
        """
        # 0.1 确保在 Home Page
        self.device.ensure_home()

        # 0.2 检查空闲队列
        idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        if idle_queues <= 0:
            self.logger.warning("没有空闲队列")
            return False

        # 0.3 检查体力
        # 根据 Diana 技能等级动态计算所需体力
        required_stamina = 25 # 默认消耗
        if "diana" in hero_config:
            diana_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
            if diana_level > 0:
                costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)
                required_stamina = costs[0] # 集结消耗
        
        # 获取当前体力 (从 DataStore 中读取)
        # 注意：这里读取的是缓存的体力值，需要确保有其他任务(如 update_stamina)定期更新它
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_stamina_check", current_stamina, required_stamina))

        if current_stamina <= required_stamina:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_stamina_low", current_stamina, required_stamina))
            return False
        
        return True

    def find_and_click_wolf(self):
        """
        1. 点击backpack.png
        2. 点击（630，120）
        3. 在屏幕上寻找check.png，如果成功则进行第四步，不成功则打断任务，返回错误。
        4. 查找并点击wolf.png
        5. 点击btn_blue.png，之后sleep（1）
        6. 点击（360，570）
        7. 点击btn_orange.png
        8. 点击btn_blue.png
        """
        IMG_BACKPACK = Template(r"assets/images/home/backpack.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/wolf/check.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_WOLF = Template(r"assets/images/wolf/wolf.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_BTN_ORANGE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_orange.png", threshold=0.9)

        # 1. 点击 backpack.png
        self.logger.info("点击背包")
        if not self.device.click(IMG_BACKPACK):
            self.logger.warning("未找到背包按钮")
            return False
        self.device.sleep(1)

        # 2. 点击（630，120）
        self.logger.info("点击指定位置 (630, 120)")
        self.device.click((630, 120))
        self.device.sleep(1)

        # 3. 在屏幕上寻找 check.png
        self.logger.info("寻找 check.png")
        if not self.device.exists(IMG_CHECK):
            self.logger.warning("未找到 check.png，任务中断")
            return False
        
        # 4. 查找并点击 wolf.png
        self.logger.info("寻找并点击 wolf.png")
        if not self.device.click(IMG_WOLF):
            self.logger.warning("未找到 wolf.png")
            return False
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

        # 5. 点击 btn_blue.png，之后 sleep(1)
        self.logger.info("点击蓝色按钮 (使用)")
        if not self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.warning("未找到蓝色按钮")
            return False
        self.device.sleep(1)

        # 6. 点击（360，570）
        self.logger.info("点击指定位置 (360, 570)")
        self.device.click((360, 570))
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

        # 7. 点击 btn_orange.png
        self.logger.info("点击橙色按钮 (搜索/确认)")
        if not self.device.click(IMG_BTN_ORANGE):
            self.logger.warning("未找到橙色按钮")
            return False
        self.device.sleep(1) # 等待地图跳转

        # 8. 点击 btn_blue.png (攻击/集结)
        self.logger.info("点击蓝色按钮 (攻击/集结)")
        # 这里可能需要等待一下，因为地图跳转和怪物显示需要时间
        self.device.sleep(1.5)
        if not self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.warning("未找到蓝色按钮 (攻击/集结)")
            return False
        self.device.sleep(1)

        return True

    def prepare_combat(self, use_one_soldier, hero_config, fallback_policy):
        """
        战斗准备：设置兵力，选择英雄
        """
        # 验证是否在战斗页面 (实际逻辑中需要确保已进入)
        self.verify_combat_page()
        

        
        # 解析英雄列表
        target_heroes = []
        if hero_config == "diana":
            target_heroes = ["diana"]
        elif hero_config == "diana_fahd":
            target_heroes = ["diana", "fahd"]
            
        # 选择英雄
        if target_heroes:
            try:
                self.select_heroes(target_heroes)
            except Exception as e:
                if fallback_policy == "wait_1h":
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_hero_fail_wait", e))
                    # 设置下次运行时间推迟1小时 (通过返回 False 或修改 interval，这里暂时返回 False 中断)
                    # 实际应用中可能需要更复杂的调度控制
                    return False
                else:
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_hero_fail_default", e))
                    # 选将失败，可能停留在英雄选择界面，需要按 BACK 返回出击页面
                    self.device.keyevent("BACK")
                    self.device.sleep(0.5)
                    # 继续执行，不选将即为默认 (或者说不使用英雄)
        
        # 设置兵力
        if use_one_soldier:
            self.set_one_soldier(True)
        
        return True

    def start_rally(self):
        """
        点击集结按钮
        """
        # 1. 检查体力是否足够 (使用 check_stamina_sufficient 检测红色警示)
        # 注意：这里是在战斗配置页面进行的最后一次检查，防止体力突然不足
        if not self.check_stamina_sufficient():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_rally_stamina_low"))
            
            # 返回主页
            self.device.ensure_home()
            
            # 将任务注册到5分钟后进行
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            return False

        # 2. 点击出击按钮
        # 暂时使用 btn_blue.png 作为出击按钮
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        self.logger.info("点击出击/集结按钮")
        if self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.device.sleep(0.5)
            # 验证是否成功 (verify_attack_success)
            return self.verify_attack_success()
        else:
            self.logger.warning("未找到出击按钮")
            return False

# tasks/daily/alliance.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class AllianceBaseTask(BaseTask):
    """
    Base class for Alliance tasks to share assets and navigation logic.
    """
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # --- Assets ---
        self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/home/alliance.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_TECH_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/tech.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_THUMB_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/thumb.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_CHEST_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/chest.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Navigation
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)

    def enter_alliance_view(self):
        """
        Navigate to Alliance View
        """
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON):
            self.logger.info("Clicking Alliance Icon")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(2.0)
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error("Alliance Icon not found")
            return False

class AllianceTechTask(AllianceBaseTask):
    # UI Configuration
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Tech Donation",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Alliance"
        }
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "AllianceTechTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 90 # Low priority

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 14400 # 4 hours

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(">>> Starting Tech Donation")
        
        if not self.enter_alliance_view():
            return False

        # Click Tech
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_TECH_ICON):
            self.logger.info("Clicking Tech Icon")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_TECH_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.warning("Tech Icon not found")
            return False

        # Check Thumb/Donation available
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_THUMB_ICON):
            self.logger.info("Found Recommended Tech (Thumb), clicking...")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_THUMB_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            # Long Press Donation Button
            self.logger.info("Long pressing donation button...")
            self.device.swipe((470, 1020), (470, 1020), duration=6.0)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.info("No Recommended Tech found, skipping donation.")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return True

class AllianceChestTask(AllianceBaseTask):
    # UI Configuration
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Chest Collection",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Alliance"
        }
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "AllianceChestTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 90 # Low priority

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 28800 # 8 hours

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(">>> Starting Chest Collection")
        
        if not self.enter_alliance_view():
            return False

        # Click Chest
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_CHEST_ICON):
            self.logger.info("Clicking Chest Icon")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_CHEST_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.warning("Chest Icon not found")
            return False

        # Sequence from MD
        # C_Pos1: Click (595, 395)
        self.device.click((595, 395))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos2: Click (572, 1187) x2
        self.device.click((572, 1187))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((572, 1187))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos3: Click (87, 385)
        self.device.click((87, 385))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos4: Click (321, 1193) x2
        self.device.click((321, 1193))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((321, 1193))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos5: Click (350, 140) x2 (Close/Back?)
        self.device.click((350, 140))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((350, 140))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        self.device.ensure_home()
        return True

# tasks/daily/intel.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from airtest.core.api import Template
from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class IntelTask(CombatTask):
    # UI Configuration
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Intel Task",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        },
        {
            "key": "desc",
            "type": "label",
            "label": "cfg_intel_desc"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # --- Assets ---
        # Intel Entry
        self.IMG_INTEL_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/home/intel.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Task Icons (Shape Only - Using White/High Contrast version)
        self.ICON_THRESHOLD = 0.85 # Slightly lower for shape matching across colors
        
        self.ICONS = {
            "Tavern": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_tavern_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
            "Tent": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_tent_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
            "Beast": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_beast_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
        }

        # Task Execution
        self.IMG_TAVERN_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/intel/tavern_check.png", threshold=0.8)
        
        # Buttons
        self.IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_GREEN = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_green.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_orange.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_EQUAL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/equal.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Navigation
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)

        # Stamina/Red Dot Checks
        # These need to be defined or we use pixel checks. 
        # For now, assuming pixel checks are done via device.get_pixel or similar if coordinates known,
        # or we use small templates if provided. 
        # The MD mentions "Check Red Pixel". We'll implement helper for that.

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "IntelTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 60

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        # 00:02, 08:02, 16:02 UTC
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        targets = [0, 8, 16]
        next_run = None
        
        for h in targets:
            target_time = now.replace(hour=h, minute=2, second=0, microsecond=0)
            if target_time > now:
                next_run = target_time
                break
                
        if not next_run:
            # Tomorrow 00:02
            next_run = now.replace(hour=0, minute=2, second=0, microsecond=0) + timedelta(days=1)
            
        # Return seconds until next run
        return (next_run - now).total_seconds()

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_start"))
        
        # --- 0. Pre-checks ---
        if not self._check_stamina_and_queue():
            return False

        # --- 1. Navigate to Intel ---
        if not self._navigate_to_intel():
            return False

        # --- 2. Batch Scan & Execute Loop ---
        while True:
            # 2.1 Batch Scan
            task_list = self._scan_all_targets()
            
            if not task_list:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_finished", "No more tasks found"))
                break
                
            self.logger.info(f"Found {len(task_list)} tasks: {[t[0] for t in task_list]}")
            
            # 2.2 Loop Execution
            for task_type, pos in task_list:
                self.logger.info(f"Executing {task_type} at {pos}")
                
                # Click the task icon
                self.device.click(pos)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
                
                # Dispatch
                success = False
                if task_type == "Tavern":
                    success = self._handle_tavern()
                elif task_type == "Tent":
                    success = self._handle_tent()
                elif task_type == "Beast":
                    success = self._handle_beast()
                
                if not success:
                    # If a task failed (e.g. stamina low, error), we might need to stop or return
                    # Based on MD, most failures lead to GoHomeError -> return False
                    # But some might just be skip? 
                    # For now, if handle returns False, we assume it handled the exit/logging
                    return False

                if not self._navigate_to_intel():
                    return False
                self.device.sleep(1.0) # Wait for view to settle

        # --- 3. Finish & Return Home ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_finished", "All tasks completed"))
        self.device.ensure_home()
        return True

    def _check_stamina_and_queue(self):
        # 1. Check Stamina
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 120)
        if current_stamina < 10:
            delay_min = (10 - current_stamina) * 5
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_stamina_low", current_stamina, 10))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            return False
            
        # 2. Check Queue
        idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        if idle_queues == 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_idle_count", 0))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=600)
            return False
            
        return True

    def _navigate_to_intel(self):
        """
        Navigate to Intel page and reset view.
        This is used both for initial entry and for returning after a task.
        It ensures the view state is consistent (reset) for coordinate reuse.
        """
        # 1. Check if we are already in Wild (Intel icon visible)
        # If we just finished a task, we are likely in Wild.
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON):
            self.logger.info("Already in Wild, clicking Intel icon")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
            # Reset View (Critical for coordinate consistency)
            self._click_reset_pixel()
            return True

        # 2. If not, ensure we are at Home (or Wild) to start fresh
        # This handles cases where we might be in a sub-menu or weird state
        self.device.ensure_home()
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

        # 3. Check if in Wild (Intel icon is only visible in Wild/Map view)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_switch_map"))
                self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
            else:
                # If neither Town button nor Go Map button is visible, we might be lost
                pass

        # 4. Click Intel Icon
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_search"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
            
            # 5. Reset View (Critical for coordinate consistency)
            self._click_reset_pixel()
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_enter_fail"))
            return False

        return True

    def _click_reset_pixel(self):
        # Click specific coordinate twice to reset view
        # (360, 1120) from previous code
        self.device.click((360, 1120))
        self.device.sleep(0.5)
        self.device.click((360, 1120))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

    def _scan_all_targets(self):
        """
        Snapshot once, then match all 3 types.
        Returns list of (Type, Pos)
        """
        try:
            # 1. Snapshot (Using DeviceOperator wrapper)
            # This saves to file and returns path
            screen_path = self.device.snapshot() 
            if not screen_path:
                self.logger.error("Snapshot failed")
                return []
                
            # 2. Read Image
            screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
            if screen is None:
                self.logger.error(f"Failed to read snapshot: {screen_path}")
                return []
                
            targets = []
            
            # 3. Match All In
            for task_type, template in self.ICONS.items():
                # match_all_in returns list of results (dicts or coordinates depending on version, usually dicts with 'result')
                results = template.match_all_in(screen)
                
                if results:
                    for res in results:
                        # Airtest match_all_in returns list of dicts: {'result': (x,y), 'rectangle': ..., 'confidence': ...}
                        if isinstance(res, dict) and 'result' in res:
                            pos = res['result']
                            targets.append((task_type, pos))
                        elif isinstance(res, (tuple, list)):
                            # Just in case it returns raw coordinates
                            targets.append((task_type, res))
            
            return targets
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Scan failed: {e}")
            return []

    def _post_task_sequence(self):
        # Ensure we are back at Intel (should be handled by task return, but double check)
        # Then click reset pixel
        self._click_reset_pixel()

    def _handle_tavern(self):
        self.logger.info("Handling Tavern Task")
        # 1. Click Blue (Start)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info("Tavern: Blue button not found (Task in progress?), skipping...")
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop
            
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. Check Red (Stamina)
        # Region: (365, 628, 400, 652)
        if not self.check_stamina_sufficient((365, 628, 400, 652)):
            self.logger.info("Tavern: Low Stamina detected")
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            req_stamina = 10
            delay_min = max(1, (req_stamina - current_stamina) * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

        # 3. Click blue (Using Region)
        # Region: (365, 628)
        self.device.click((365, 628))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # 4. click green
        if not self.device.click((470, 1200)): return False
        
        # 5. Scan for Check
        self.logger.info("Scanning for Tavern Check...")
        start_time = time.time()
        found = False
        while time.time() - start_time < 60:
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_TAVERN_CHECK):
                found = True
                break
            self.device.sleep(1.3)
            
        if found:
            self.device.press_back()
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            self._update_stats(cost=10)
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error("Tavern Scan Timeout")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

    def _handle_tent(self):
        self.logger.info("Handling Tent Task")
        # 1. Click Blue
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info("Tent: Blue button not found (Task in progress?), skipping...")
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop

        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. Check Red
        # Region: (365, 628, 400, 652)
        if not self.check_stamina_sufficient((365, 628, 400, 652)):
            self.logger.info("Tent: Low Stamina detected")
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            req_stamina = 12
            delay_min = max(1, (req_stamina - current_stamina) * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
            
        # 3. Click Green (Using Region)
        # Region: (365, 628)
        self.device.click((365, 628))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        self._update_stats(cost=12)
        return True

    def _handle_beast(self):
        self.logger.info("Handling Beast Task")
        
        # 1. Check Queue
        idle_queues = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0)
        if idle_queues == 0:
            self.logger.info("Beast: No queue")
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

        # 2. Click Blue
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info("Beast: Blue button not found (Task in progress?), skipping...")
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop

        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 3. Click Orange
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 4. Check Red (Stamina)
        # Region: (546, 1213, 607, 1246)
        if not self.check_stamina_sufficient((546, 1213, 607, 1246)):
            self.logger.info("Beast: Low Stamina detected")
            
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            req_stamina = 10 # As per instruction
            delay_min = max(1, (req_stamina - current_stamina) * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
            
        # 5. Calculate Cost (High Stamina)
        # Determine cost before action
        cost = self.calculate_cost(mode="wild")
        
        # 6. Click Equal
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 7. Click Blue2 (March) (Using Region)
        # Region: (546, 1213)
        self.device.click((546, 1213))
        self.device.sleep(2.0)
        
        # 8. Check Home (Success)
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON) or self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            # Success
            self._update_stats(cost=cost, queue=1)
            
            # Check Queue After
            if self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0) == 0:
                self.logger.info("Beast: Queue full after attack")
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                self.device.ensure_home()
                return False
                
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error("Beast: Failed to return home")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

    def _update_stats(self, cost=0, queue=0):
        if cost > 0:
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            new_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - cost)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_stamina)
            self.logger.info(f"Deducting stamina: {cost}, Remaining: {new_stamina}")

        if queue > 0:
            current_idle = self.sidebar.idle_march_count
            new_idle = max(0, current_idle - queue)
            self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", new_idle)
            self.sidebar.idle_march_count = new_idle
            self.logger.info(f"Deducting idle queue: {queue}, Remaining: {new_idle}")

# tools/resource_loader.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import cv2
import numpy as np
from airtest.core.api import Template as AirtestTemplate
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

# ==============================================================================
# 🔑 解密密钥配置 (必须与加密时的密钥完全一致！)
# ==============================================================================
# 请把 encrypt_tool.py 生成的 b'xxx...' 完整填入下面
# 示例: DECRYPTION_KEY = b'A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8I9j0K1l2M3n4O5p6Q7r8S9t0U1v='
DECRYPTION_KEY = b'gMN3RKaTDbu38pLlA3yah7dZYD8XhxBOWeAtY5eNo64=' 

# 初始化解密器
try:
    cipher = Fernet(DECRYPTION_KEY)
except Exception as e:
    print(f"[ResourceLoader] ⚠️ 密钥格式错误或未配置: {e}")
    cipher = None

class MemoryTemplate(AirtestTemplate):
    """
    安全模版类：
    1. 接收加密的二进制流
    2. 内存解密 (AES)
    3. 内存解码 (OpenCV)
    4. 不落地硬盘
    """
    def __init__(self, encrypted_bytes, threshold=None, target_pos=5, record_pos=None, resolution=(), **kwargs):
        self._img_content = None
        
        if encrypted_bytes and cipher:
            try:
                # --- 第1步：解密 ---
                # 如果这里报错，说明密钥不对，或者文件本身没有加密
                decrypted_bytes = cipher.decrypt(encrypted_bytes)
                
                # --- 第2步：解码图片 ---
                # 将二进制流解码为 OpenCV 图像格式
                nparr = np.frombuffer(decrypted_bytes, np.uint8)
                self._img_content = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)
                
                if self._img_content is not None:
                    # [可选] 调试时打印，发布时可注释掉
                    # print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ✅ 图片解密加载成功! 尺寸: {self._img_content.shape}")
                    pass
                else:
                    print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 解密成功但图片解码失败 (数据损坏?)")

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 加载失败: {e}")
                # 常见原因：密钥不匹配，或者KeyAuth填的是未加密的直链
        
        elif not cipher:
            print("[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 致命错误：解密密钥未配置！")
        else:
            print("[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 错误：接收到的数据为空！")

        # 欺骗父类构造函数，传入虚拟路径防止报错
        super().__init__(filename="MEMORY_ASSET", threshold=threshold, target_pos=target_pos, record_pos=record_pos, resolution=resolution, **kwargs)

    def _imread(self):
        # 核心：重写读取方法，直接返回内存中的 OpenCV 图片对象
        return self._img_content

class ResourceLoader:
    _instance = None
    _keyauth_app = None
    _cache = {}  # 缓存下载好的加密数据，避免重复下载

    def __new__(cls):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = super(ResourceLoader, cls).__new__(cls)
        return cls._instance

    def initialize(self, keyauth_app):
        """注入登录成功的 keyauth 实例"""
        self._keyauth_app = keyauth_app

    def get_template(self, file_id, threshold=0.9, record_pos=None, resolution=()):
        """
        获取安全模版：先查缓存，没有则下载
        """
        if not self._keyauth_app:
            print("[ResourceLoader] KeyAuth not initialized!")
            return None

        # 1. 检查缓存 (缓存里存的是加密的 bytes)
        if file_id in self._cache:
            # print(f"[ResourceLoader] 使用缓存 ID: {file_id}")
            encrypted_bytes = self._cache[file_id]
        else:
            # 2. 下载
            print(f"[ResourceLoader] 正在从云端下载资源 ID: {file_id} ...")
            try:
                # KeyAuth 的 file 方法会自动处理 Direct Link 下载
                encrypted_bytes = self._keyauth_app.file(file_id)
                
                # 简单的有效性检查
                if not encrypted_bytes or len(encrypted_bytes) < 100:
                    print(f"[ResourceLoader] ⚠️ 下载数据异常，长度仅 {len(encrypted_bytes) if encrypted_bytes else 0} bytes")
                    # 可能是 404 或 401 错误文本
                    return None
                    
                self._cache[file_id] = encrypted_bytes
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ResourceLoader] Download Exception: {e}")
                return None

        # 3. 创建并返回内存模版 (解密过程在 MemoryTemplate 内部发生)
        return MemoryTemplate(encrypted_bytes, threshold=threshold, record_pos=record_pos, resolution=resolution)

import os
