try:
    if os.name == 'nt':
        import win32security  # get sid (WIN only)
    import requests  # https requests
except ModuleNotFoundError:
    print("Exception when importing modules")
    print("Installing necessary modules....")
    if os.path.isfile("requirements.txt"):
        os.system("pip install -r requirements.txt")
    else:
        if os.name == 'nt':
            os.system("pip install pywin32")
        os.system("pip install requests")
    print("Modules installed!")
    time.sleep(1.5)
    os._exit(1)


class api:

    name = ownerid = version = hash_to_check = ""

    def __init__(self, name, ownerid, version, hash_to_check):
        if len(ownerid) != 10:
            print("Visit https://keyauth.cc/app/, copy Pthon code, and replace code in main.py with that")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
    
        self.name = name

        self.ownerid = ownerid

        self.version = version
        self.hash_to_check = hash_to_check
        self.init()

    sessionid = enckey = ""
    initialized = False

    def init(self):
        if self.sessionid != "":
            print("You've already initialized!")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "init",
            "ver": self.version,
            "hash": self.hash_to_check,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid
        }

        response = self.__do_request(post_data)

        if response == "KeyAuth_Invalid":
            print("The application doesn't exist")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

        json = jsond.loads(response)

        if json["message"] == "invalidver":
            if json["download"] != "":
                print("New Version Available")
                download_link = json["download"]
                os.system(f"start {download_link}")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)
            else:
                print("Invalid Version, Contact owner to add download link to latest app version")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

        if not json["success"]:
            print(json["message"])
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

        self.sessionid = json["sessionid"]
        self.initialized = True

    def register(self, user, password, license, hwid=None):
        self.checkinit()
        if hwid is None:
            hwid = others.get_hwid()

        post_data = {
            "type": "register",
            "username": user,
            "pass": password,
            "key": license,
            "hwid": hwid,
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid
        }

        response = self.__do_request(post_data)

        json = jsond.loads(response)

        if json["success"]:
            print(json["message"])
            self.__load_user_data(json["info"])
        else:
            print(json["message"])
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

    def enable2fa(self, code=None):
        self.checkinit()
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "2faenable",
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid,
            "code": code
        }       
        
        response = self.__do_request(post_data)
        
        json = jsond.loads(response)
        
        if json["success"]:
            if code is None:
                # First request: Display the 2FA secret code
                print(f"Your 2FA secret code is: {json['2fa']['secret_code']}")
                qr_code = json['2fa']['QRCode']
                self.display_qr_code(qr_code)
                code_input = input("Enter the 6 digit 2fa code to enable 2fa: ")
                self.enable2fa(code_input);
            else:
                # Second request: Confirm successful 2FA activation
                print("2FA has been successfully enabled!")
                time.sleep(3)
        else:
            print(f"Error: {json['message']}")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
            
    def disable2fa(self, code=None):
        self.checkinit()
        
        code = input("Enter the 6 digit 2fa code to disable 2fa: ")
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "2fadisable",
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid,
            "code": code
        }
        
        response = self.__do_request(post_data)
        
        json = jsond.loads(response)
        
        print(json['message'])
        time.sleep(3)
        
            
    def display_qr_code(self, qr_code_url):
            # Generate QR code image
            qr = qrcode.QRCode(
                version=1,
                error_correction=qrcode.constants.ERROR_CORRECT_L,
                box_size=10,
                border=4,
            )

            # Add the QR code URL data
            qr.add_data(qr_code_url)
            qr.make(fit=True)

            # Create an image from the QR code
            img = qr.make_image(fill='black', back_color='white')

            # Display the QR code image
            img.show()            
            
    def __do_request(self, post_data):
        try:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://keyauth.win/api/1.3/", data=post_data, timeout=10
            )

            if post_data["type"] == "log" or post_data["type"] == "file" or post_data["type"] == "2faenable" or post_data["type"] == "2fadisable":
                return response.text

            # Get the signature and timestamp from the headers
            signature = response.headers.get("x-signature-ed25519")
            timestamp = response.headers.get("x-signature-timestamp")

            if not signature or not timestamp:
                print("Missing headers for signature verification.")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            server_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(timestamp), timezone.utc)
            current_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            
            #print(f"Server Timestamp (UTC seconds): {timestamp}")
            #print(f"Server Time (UTC seconds): {server_time.timestamp()}")
            #print(f"Current Time (UTC seconds): {current_time.timestamp()}")

            buffer_seconds = 5
            time_difference = current_time - server_time

            if time_difference > timedelta(seconds=20 + buffer_seconds):
                print("Timestamp is too old (exceeded 20 seconds + buffer).")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            if not verify_key(response.text.encode('utf-8'), signature, timestamp, '5586b4bc69c7a4b487e4563a4cd96afd39140f919bd31cea7d1c6a1e8439422b'):
                print("Signature checksum failed. Request was tampered with or session ended most likely.")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            return response.text

        except requests.exceptions.Timeout: 
            print("Request timed out. Server is probably down/slow at the moment")
                
            
    class application_data_class:
        numUsers = numKeys = app_ver = customer_panel = onlineUsers = ""

    class user_data_class:
        username = ip = hwid = expires = createdate = lastlogin = subscription = subscriptions = ""

    user_data = user_data_class()
    app_data = application_data_class()

    def __load_app_data(self, data):
        self.app_data.numUsers = data["numUsers"]
        self.app_data.numKeys = data["numKeys"]
        self.app_data.app_ver = data["version"]
        self.app_data.customer_panel = data["customerPanelLink"]
        self.app_data.onlineUsers = data["numOnlineUsers"]

    def __load_user_data(self, data):
        self.user_data.username = data["username"]
        self.user_data.ip = data["ip"]
        self.user_data.hwid = data["hwid"] or "N/A"
        self.user_data.expires = data["subscriptions"][0]["expiry"]
        self.user_data.createdate = data["createdate"]
        self.user_data.lastlogin = data["lastlogin"]
        self.user_data.subscription = data["subscriptions"][0]["subscription"]
        self.user_data.subscriptions = data["subscriptions"]


class others:
    @staticmethod
    def get_hwid():
        if platform.system() == "Linux":
            with open("/etc/machine-id") as f:
                hwid = f.read()
                return hwid
        elif platform.system() == 'Windows':
            winuser = os.getlogin()
            sid = win32security.LookupAccountName(None, winuser)[0]  # You can also use WMIC (better than SID, some users had problems with WMIC)
            hwid = win32security.ConvertSidToStringSid(sid)
            return hwid
            '''
            cmd = subprocess.Popen(
                "wmic useraccount where name='%username%' get sid",
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                shell=True,
            )

            (suppost_sid, error) = cmd.communicate()

            suppost_sid = suppost_sid.split(b"\n")[1].strip()

            return suppost_sid.decode()

            ^^ HOW TO DO IT USING WMIC
            '''
        elif platform.system() == 'Darwin':
            output = subprocess.Popen("ioreg -l | grep IOPlatformSerialNumber", stdout=subprocess.PIPE, shell=True).communicate()[0]
            serial = output.decode().split('=', 1)[1].replace(' ', '')
            hwid = serial[1:-2]
            return hwid

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QLineEdit, QComboBox, QPushButton, QMessageBox, QFormLayout, QSpinBox
)
from airtest.core.android.adb import ADB
import os
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class AddInstanceDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(get_text("title_add_instance"))
        self.resize(400, 200)
        self.setup_ui()
        self.refresh_devices()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        form = QFormLayout()
        
        # 实例名称
        self.line_name = QLineEdit()
        self.line_name.setPlaceholderText("e.g. Main, Alt1...")
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_name"), self.line_name)

        # 设备序列号
        hbox_serial = QHBoxLayout()
        self.combo_serial = QComboBox()
        self.combo_serial.setEditable(True)
        self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText("Serial/IP:Port")
        
        btn_refresh = QPushButton("🔄")
        btn_refresh.setToolTip(get_text("btn_refresh_adb"))
        btn_refresh.setFixedWidth(40)
        btn_refresh.clicked.connect(self.refresh_devices)
        
        hbox_serial.addWidget(self.combo_serial)
        hbox_serial.addWidget(btn_refresh)
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_address"), hbox_serial)

        # Diana 等级
        self.spin_diana = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_diana.setRange(0, 5)
        self.spin_diana.setValue(0)
        self.spin_diana.setSuffix(" Lv")
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_diana"), self.spin_diana)

        # 行军队列
        self.spin_march = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_march.setRange(1, 6)
        self.spin_march.setValue(5)
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_march_slots"), self.spin_march)
        
        layout.addLayout(form)

        # 按钮
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_ok = QPushButton("确定")
        btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_cancel = QPushButton("取消")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_ok)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def refresh_devices(self):
        self.combo_serial.clear()
        try:
            # 1. 获取配置的 ADB 路径
            from config import global_config
            adb_path = global_config.get("adb_path", "")
            use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
            
            # 如果使用内置，尝试构建路径 (这里假设在当前目录下有 adb/adb.exe)
            if use_internal:
                # 简单的内置路径推断，实际可能需要更严谨的判断
                if os.path.exists("adb/adb.exe"):
                    adb_path = os.path.abspath("adb/adb.exe")
                else:
                    # 如果没找到内置的，就让 Airtest 自己去找
                    adb_path = None
            
            # 2. 初始化 ADB
            if adb_path and os.path.exists(adb_path):
                adb = ADB(adb_path=adb_path)
            else:
                adb = ADB() # 尝试自动查找

            # 3. 获取设备列表
            devices = adb.devices() # returns list of (serial, status)
            
            count = 0
            for serial, status in devices:
                if status == 'device':
                    self.combo_serial.addItem(serial)
                    count += 1
            
            if count == 0:
                self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText("未检测到在线设备")
            else:
                self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText("请选择设备...")
                self.combo_serial.showPopup() # 自动弹出列表方便选择

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error refreshing devices: {e}")
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "ADB 错误", f"无法获取设备列表:\n{e}")

    def get_data(self):
        return (
            self.line_name.text().strip(),
            self.combo_serial.currentText().strip(),
            self.spin_diana.value(),
            self.spin_march.value()
        )

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtCore import Slot, Signal, Qt, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve, QParallelAnimationGroup, QAbstractAnimation, QSize, QEvent
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QToolButton, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QFrame, QScrollArea, QSizePolicy, QCheckBox, QLabel

class AutoHeightScrollArea(QScrollArea):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
        self.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        self.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setWidgetResizable(True)

    def eventFilter(self, o, e):
        if o == self.widget() and e.type() == QEvent.Resize:
            self.updateGeometry()
        return super().eventFilter(o, e)

    def setWidget(self, widget):
        super().setWidget(widget)
        if widget:
            widget.installEventFilter(self)

    def sizeHint(self):
        if self.widget():
            # Add a small buffer to prevent cutting off content
            h = self.widget().sizeHint().height() + 5
            if self.maximumHeight() < 16777215:
                h = min(h, self.maximumHeight())
            return QSize(super().sizeHint().width(), h)
        return QSize(0, 0)

class CollapsibleBox(QWidget):
    # Signal when the content visibility toggles (expanded/collapsed)
    toggled_state = Signal(bool)
    # Signal when the checkbox is toggled (enabled/disabled)
    toggled_enable = Signal(bool)
    # Signal when the size of the box changes (e.g. after animation)
    size_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, title="", parent=None, checkable=False):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.is_checkable = checkable
        
        # --- Header ---
        self.header_widget = QWidget()
        self.header_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.header_widget)
        self.header_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.header_layout.setSpacing(5)

        # 1. Arrow Button
        self.toggle_button = QToolButton()
        self.toggle_button.setStyleSheet("QToolButton { border: none; background: transparent; }")
        self.toggle_button.setToolButtonStyle(Qt.ToolButtonIconOnly)
        self.toggle_button.setArrowType(Qt.RightArrow)
        self.toggle_button.setCheckable(True)
        self.toggle_button.setChecked(False)
        self.toggle_button.clicked.connect(self.on_toggle)
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.toggle_button)

        # 2. Checkbox (Optional)
        self.checkbox = None
        if checkable:
            self.checkbox = QCheckBox()
            self.checkbox.toggled.connect(self.on_checkbox_toggled)
            self.header_layout.addWidget(self.checkbox)

        # 3. Title Label
        self.title_label = QLabel(title)
        self.title_label.mousePressEvent = self.on_title_clicked
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.title_label)
        
        self.header_layout.addStretch()

        # --- Content ---
        self.toggle_animation = QParallelAnimationGroup(self)
        self.toggle_animation.finished.connect(self.on_animation_finished)

        self.content_area = AutoHeightScrollArea()
        self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(0)
        self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(0)
        self.content_area.setVisible(False)

        # --- Main Layout ---
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setSpacing(0)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        lay.addWidget(self.header_widget)
        lay.addWidget(self.content_area)

        self.anim_max = QPropertyAnimation(self.content_area, b"maximumHeight")
        self.anim_min = QPropertyAnimation(self.content_area, b"minimumHeight")
        
        self.anim_max.valueChanged.connect(self.on_animation_value_changed)
        self.anim_min.valueChanged.connect(self.on_animation_value_changed)
        
        self.toggle_animation.addAnimation(self.anim_max)
        self.toggle_animation.addAnimation(self.anim_min)

    def on_animation_value_changed(self, value):
        self.content_area.updateGeometry()
        self.updateGeometry()
        self.size_changed.emit()

    def on_title_clicked(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.toggle_button.click()

    @Slot()
    def on_toggle(self):
        if self.toggle_animation.state() == QAbstractAnimation.Running:
            # If animating, ignore or handle gracefully.
            self.toggle_button.setChecked(not self.toggle_button.isChecked())
            return

        checked = self.toggle_button.isChecked()
        self.toggle_button.setArrowType(Qt.DownArrow if checked else Qt.RightArrow)
        
        # Always use Forward because we explicitly set Start/End values for both directions
        self.toggle_animation.setDirection(QPropertyAnimation.Forward)

        # Calculate content height
        content_height = 0
        if self.content_area.widget():
            self.content_area.widget().adjustSize()
            content_height = self.content_area.widget().sizeHint().height() + 5

        if checked:
            # Expanding: 0 -> content_height
            self.content_area.setVisible(True)
            self.anim_max.setStartValue(0)
            self.anim_max.setEndValue(content_height)
            self.anim_min.setStartValue(0)
            self.anim_min.setEndValue(content_height)
        else:
            # Collapsing: current -> 0
            # Snap to current height first (in case it was unlocked/MAX)
            current_h = self.content_area.height()
            self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(current_h)
            self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(current_h)
            
            self.anim_max.setStartValue(current_h)
            self.anim_max.setEndValue(0)
            self.anim_min.setStartValue(current_h)
            self.anim_min.setEndValue(0)

        self.toggle_animation.start()
        self.toggled_state.emit(checked)

    @Slot()
    def on_animation_finished(self):
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            # Expanded: Unlock height to allow dynamic resizing of content
            self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(16777215) # QWIDGETSIZE_MAX
            self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(0)
        else:
            self.content_area.setVisible(False)
        
        # Ensure the content area (scroll area) updates its geometry/size hint
        self.content_area.updateGeometry()
        # Trigger layout update
        self.updateGeometry()
        self.size_changed.emit()

    @Slot(bool)
    def on_checkbox_toggled(self, checked):
        self.toggled_enable.emit(checked)

    def setContentLayout(self, layout):
        widget = QWidget()
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.content_area.setWidget(widget)
        
        # Connect signals from child CollapsibleBoxes
        self._connect_child_signals(layout)

        # Initial setup
        widget.adjustSize()
        
        self.anim_max.setDuration(300)
        self.anim_max.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.InOutQuad)
        self.anim_min.setDuration(300)
        self.anim_min.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.InOutQuad)

    def _connect_child_signals(self, layout):
        for i in range(layout.count()):
            item = layout.itemAt(i)
            if item.widget():
                widget = item.widget()
                if isinstance(widget, CollapsibleBox):
                    widget.size_changed.connect(self.refresh_height)
                elif widget.layout():
                    self._connect_child_signals(widget.layout())
            elif item.layout():
                self._connect_child_signals(item.layout())

    @Slot()
    def refresh_height(self):
        # If we are expanded, we need to update our geometry to accommodate child changes
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            self.content_area.widget().adjustSize()
            self.content_area.updateGeometry()
            self.updateGeometry()
            self.size_changed.emit()

    # --- Compatibility Methods ---
    
    def setTitle(self, title):
        self.title_label.setText(title)

    def setCheckable(self, checkable):
        pass 

    def setChecked(self, checked):
        if self.checkbox:
            self.checkbox.setChecked(checked)

    def isChecked(self):
        if self.checkbox:
            return self.checkbox.isChecked()
        return False

    def collapse(self):
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            self.toggle_button.click()

    def expand(self):
        if not self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            self.toggle_button.click()

# ui/edit_instance_dialog.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QFormLayout, QLineEdit, 
    QDialogButtonBox, QSpinBox, QLabel
)
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class EditInstanceDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, current_name="", current_serial="", current_diana=0, current_march_slots=5):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(get_text("title_edit_instance"))
        self.resize(350, 250)
        
        # 记录原始数据
        self.old_name = current_name
        self.old_serial = current_serial
        self.old_diana = current_diana
        self.old_march_slots = current_march_slots
        
        self.setup_ui()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 表单区域
        form = QFormLayout()
        
        # 1. 名字
        self.line_name = QLineEdit(self.old_name)
        self.line_name.setPlaceholderText("New Name")
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_name"), self.line_name)
        
        # 2. 地址
        self.line_serial = QLineEdit(self.old_serial)
        self.line_serial.setPlaceholderText("e.g. 127.0.0.1:5555")
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_address"), self.line_serial)
        
        # 3. Diana 等级 (新增)
        self.spin_diana = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_diana.setRange(0, 5) # 0表示没有，1-5是等级
        self.spin_diana.setValue(int(self.old_diana))
        self.spin_diana.setSuffix(" Lv")
        # 加个小提示
        lbl_tip = QLabel(get_text("tip_diana"))
        lbl_tip.setStyleSheet("color: gray; font-size: 9pt;")
        
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_diana"), self.spin_diana)
        form.addRow("", lbl_tip) # 占位显示提示

        # 4. 行军队列 (新增)
        self.spin_march = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_march.setRange(1, 6)
        self.spin_march.setValue(int(self.old_march_slots))
        
        lbl_tip_march = QLabel(get_text("tip_march_slots"))
        lbl_tip_march.setStyleSheet("color: gray; font-size: 9pt;")
        
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_march_slots"), self.spin_march)
        form.addRow("", lbl_tip_march)
        
        layout.addLayout(form)

        # 按钮区域 (确定/取消)
        buttons = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.Ok | QDialogButtonBox.Cancel)
        buttons.accepted.connect(self.accept)
        buttons.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        layout.addWidget(buttons)

    def get_data(self):
        """返回: (name, serial, diana_level, march_slots)"""
        return (
            self.line_name.text().strip(),
            self.line_serial.text().strip(),
            self.spin_diana.value(),
            self.spin_march.value()
        )

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QCheckBox, 
    QSpinBox, QLabel, QGridLayout, QScrollArea, QFrame, QPushButton
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QSize
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon
from game.hero_manager import HeroManager
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class CollapsibleBox(QWidget):
    """自定义折叠控件"""
    def __init__(self, title="", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.toggle_button = QPushButton(title)
        self.toggle_button.setCheckable(True)
        self.toggle_button.setChecked(False) # 默认收起
        self.toggle_button.setStyleSheet("text-align: left; font-weight: bold; padding: 5px;")
        self.toggle_button.toggled.connect(self.on_toggled)

        self.content_area = QWidget()
        self.content_area.setVisible(False) # 默认隐藏
        
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.toggle_button)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.content_area)

    def on_toggled(self, checked):
        self.content_area.setVisible(checked)
        arrow = "▼" if checked else "▶"
        self.toggle_button.setText(f"{arrow} {self.toggle_button.text()[2:]}")

    def set_content_layout(self, layout):
        self.content_area.setLayout(layout)
        
    def set_title(self, title):
        arrow = "▼" if self.toggle_button.isChecked() else "▶"
        self.toggle_button.setText(f"{arrow} {title}")

class HeroManagementWidget(QWidget):
    """
    英雄管理组件
    使用折叠菜单展示英雄列表，允许用户配置拥有状态和队列信息。
    """

    # 英雄分组定义 (用于 UI 展示)
    HERO_GROUPS = {
        "Gen 4": ["rosa", "alcar", "margot"],
        "Gen 3": ["jaeger", "eric", "petra"],
        "Gen 2": ["hilde", "zoe", "marlin"],
        "Gen 1": ["jabel", "amadeus", "helga", "saul"],
        "SR (Epic)": ["amane", "yeonwoo", "fahd", "chenko", "gordon", "diana", "howard", "quinn"],
        "R (Rare)": ["olive", "edwin", "seth", "forrest"]
    }

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.hero_manager = None
        self.widgets = {} # {hero_name: {"chk": QCheckBox, "spin": QSpinBox}}
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        # 使用自定义折叠控件包裹整个内容
        self.main_collapsible = CollapsibleBox(f"▶ {get_text('title_hero_management', '英雄状态管理')}")
        layout.addWidget(self.main_collapsible)

        # 内部布局 (放入 ScrollArea)
        scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll_area.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        
        inner_widget = QWidget()
        inner_layout = QVBoxLayout(inner_widget)
        inner_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        # 动态创建分组 (使用 CollapsibleBox 替代 QToolBox)
        for group_name, heroes in self.HERO_GROUPS.items():
            # 创建分组折叠框
            group_box = CollapsibleBox(f"▶ {group_name}")
            
            # 创建分组内容页面
            page_layout = self.create_group_page_layout(heroes)
            group_box.set_content_layout(page_layout)
            
            inner_layout.addWidget(group_box)

        # 添加弹簧，确保内容靠上
        inner_layout.addStretch()
        
        scroll_area.setWidget(inner_widget)
        
        # 创建一个包装布局给 main_collapsible
        wrapper_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        wrapper_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        # 设置最小高度，避免展开时太小
        scroll_area.setMinimumHeight(200)
        wrapper_layout.addWidget(scroll_area)
        
        self.main_collapsible.set_content_layout(wrapper_layout)

    def create_group_page_layout(self, heroes):
        """创建单个分组页面的布局"""
        # 使用 QWidget 包裹内容，以便放入 ScrollArea (如果需要) 或者直接作为布局
        # 这里直接返回布局，由 CollapsibleBox 的 content_area 使用
        
        # 为了美观，我们在每个分组里也加一个 ScrollArea，或者直接用 Grid
        # 考虑到英雄数量不多，直接用 Grid 即可。如果英雄很多，可以考虑 ScrollArea。
        # 这里为了保持一致性，我们还是用一个 Widget 包裹 Grid
        
        content_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(content_widget)
        grid.setColumnStretch(1, 1) # 让中间空白区域伸缩
        grid.setContentsMargins(10, 5, 10, 5) # 增加一点内边距

        # 表头
        lbl_name = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_name", "英雄"))
        lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_name, 0, 0)
        
        lbl_owned = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_owned", "拥有"))
        lbl_owned.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_owned, 0, 2)
        
        lbl_status = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_queue", "状态"))
        lbl_status.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_status, 0, 3)

        row = 1
        for hero_name in heroes:
            # 1. 英雄名称 (支持中文)
            hero_key = f"hero_{hero_name}"
            display_name = get_text(hero_key, hero_name.capitalize())
            lbl_name = QLabel(display_name)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0)

            # 2. 拥有状态 (CheckBox)
            chk_owned = QCheckBox()
            chk_owned.stateChanged.connect(lambda state, h=hero_name: self.on_owned_changed(h, state))
            grid.addWidget(chk_owned, row, 2, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)

            # 3. 状态信息 (可点击切换的 Button 或 Label)
            # 这里为了简化操作，使用 QPushButton，点击切换 忙碌/空闲
            btn_status = QPushButton(get_text("val_idle", "空闲"))
            btn_status.setCheckable(True) # 选中表示忙碌
            btn_status.setFixedWidth(60)
            btn_status.clicked.connect(lambda checked, h=hero_name: self.on_status_changed(h, checked))
            grid.addWidget(btn_status, row, 3)

            # 存储控件引用以便后续更新
            self.widgets[hero_name] = {
                "chk": chk_owned,
                "btn_status": btn_status
            }
            row += 1

        # 布局本身
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.addWidget(content_widget)
        
        return layout

    def set_manager(self, manager: HeroManager):
        """设置 HeroManager 实例并刷新 UI"""
        self.hero_manager = manager
        self.refresh_ui()

    def refresh_ui(self):
        """从 Manager 读取数据更新 UI"""
        if not self.hero_manager:
            return

        # 临时断开信号，防止循环触发
        self.block_signals(True)

        for hero_name, controls in self.widgets.items():
            info = self.hero_manager.get_hero_info(hero_name)
            
            # 更新 CheckBox
            is_owned = info.get("owned", False)
            controls["chk"].setChecked(is_owned)
            
            # 更新状态 Button
            state = info.get("state", "idle")
            is_busy = (state == "busy")
            
            btn = controls["btn_status"]
            btn.setChecked(is_busy)
            self.update_status_btn_style(btn, is_busy)
            
            # 如果未拥有，禁用状态切换
            btn.setEnabled(is_owned)

        self.block_signals(False)

    def update_status_btn_style(self, btn, is_busy):
        if is_busy:
            btn.setText(get_text("val_busy", "忙碌"))
            btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ffcccc; color: red; border: 1px solid red;")
        else:
            btn.setText(get_text("val_idle", "空闲"))
            btn.setStyleSheet("color: green;")

    def block_signals(self, block):
        """批量屏蔽/恢复信号"""
        for controls in self.widgets.values():
            controls["chk"].blockSignals(block)
            controls["btn_status"].blockSignals(block)

    def on_owned_changed(self, hero_name, state):
        """处理拥有状态变更"""
        if not self.hero_manager: return
        
        is_owned = (state == Qt.Checked.value)
        self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, owned=is_owned)
        
        # 联动：如果取消拥有，禁用状态按钮并重置为空闲
        controls = self.widgets.get(hero_name)
        if controls:
            btn = controls["btn_status"]
            btn.setEnabled(is_owned)
            if not is_owned:
                self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, state="idle")
                btn.setChecked(False)
                self.update_status_btn_style(btn, False)

    def on_status_changed(self, hero_name, checked):
        """处理状态变更 (点击按钮)"""
        if not self.hero_manager: return
        
        new_state = "busy" if checked else "idle"
        self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, state=new_state)
        
        # 更新按钮样式
        controls = self.widgets.get(hero_name)
        if controls:
            self.update_status_btn_style(controls["btn_status"], checked)


==================================================
FILE: .\ui\login_dialog.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QLineEdit, QPushButton, 
    QMessageBox, QHBoxLayout, QCheckBox
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class LoginDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, keyauth_app, parent=None, initial_error=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.keyauth_app = keyauth_app
        self.initial_error = initial_error
        self.setWindowTitle("KSAS Login")
        self.resize(350, 180)
        self.setup_ui()
        
        if self.initial_error:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Auto-Login Failed", f"Previous session invalid:\n{self.initial_error}")

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)

        # 标题
        lbl_title = QLabel("KSAS")
        lbl_title.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-size: 18pt; font-weight: bold; color: #00695C;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_title)

        # 输入框
        self.line_key = QLineEdit()
        self.line_key.setPlaceholderText("Enter License Key")
        self.line_key.setStyleSheet("padding: 8px; border: 1px solid #B2DFDB; border-radius: 4px;")
        layout.addWidget(self.line_key)

        # 记住卡密
        self.chk_remember = QCheckBox("Remember Key")
        self.chk_remember.setChecked(True)
        layout.addWidget(self.chk_remember)

        # 按钮
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_login = QPushButton("Login")
        self.btn_login.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #009688; 
                color: white; 
                padding: 8px; 
                border-radius: 4px; 
                font-weight: bold;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #00796B; }
        """)
        self.btn_login.clicked.connect(self.do_login)
        
        btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancel")
        btn_cancel.setStyleSheet("padding: 8px; border: 1px solid #ccc; border-radius: 4px;")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)

        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_login)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def do_login(self):
        key = self.line_key.text().strip()
        if not key:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please enter a license key.")
            return

        self.btn_login.setEnabled(False)
        self.btn_login.setText("Verifying...")
        
        try:
            # 调用 KeyAuth 登录
            # license() returns None on success, or error message string on failure
            result = self.keyauth_app.license(key)
            
            if result is None: # Success
                if self.keyauth_app.check():
                    self.accept()
                else:
                    QMessageBox.critical(self, "Login Failed", "Session check failed after license verification.")
                    self.btn_login.setEnabled(True)
                    self.btn_login.setText("Login")
            else:
                # result contains the error message from KeyAuth
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Login Failed", f"Verification failed:\n{result}")
                self.btn_login.setEnabled(True)
                self.btn_login.setText("Login")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Connection Error: {str(e)}")
            self.btn_login.setEnabled(True)
            self.btn_login.setText("Login")

    def get_key(self):
        return self.line_key.text().strip()

# ============================================================================
# 💓 KeyAuth 心跳线程 (定义在主逻辑类之前或之后均可，这里放在前面方便管理)
# ============================================================================
class KeyAuthHeartbeatThread(QThread):
    """
    KeyAuth 心跳保活线程 (带冗余重试机制)
    策略:
    1. 正常状态：每 30 分钟检测一次。
    2. 失败状态：进入重试模式，每 3 分钟检测一次。
    3. 判定死亡：连续 3 次失败后，发送死亡信号。
    """
    sig_auth_failed = Signal(str)  # 彻底失败信号

    def __init__(self, keyauth_app):
        super().__init__()
        self.api = keyauth_app
        self._is_running = True
        self.failed_attempts = 0 # 当前连续失败次数
        
        # 配置
        self.INTERVAL_NORMAL = 30 * 60  # 正常间隔 30分钟
        self.INTERVAL_RETRY = 3 * 60    # 重试间隔 3分钟
        self.MAX_RETRIES = 3            # 最大重试次数

    def run(self):
        # 刚启动时，先等待一个周期再检查（因为登录时已经检查过了）
        self.smart_sleep(self.INTERVAL_NORMAL)

        while self._is_running:
            try:
                # 执行检测
                # print(f"[Heartbeat] Checking... (Failures: {self.failed_attempts})")
                is_valid = self.api.check()
                
                if is_valid:
                    # 成功：重置计数器，等待 30 分钟
                    if self.failed_attempts > 0:
                        print("[Heartbeat] Connection restored.")
                    self.failed_attempts = 0
                    self.smart_sleep(self.INTERVAL_NORMAL)
                else:
                    # 失败：计数+1
                    self.failed_attempts += 1
                    print(f"[Heartbeat] Check failed ({self.failed_attempts}/{self.MAX_RETRIES})")
                    
                    if self.failed_attempts >= self.MAX_RETRIES:
                        # 超过最大重试次数，发送死亡信号，结束循环
                        self.sig_auth_failed.emit(f"Heartbeat failed {self.MAX_RETRIES} times continuously.")
                        break
                    else:
                        # 还没死透，等待 3 分钟重试
                        self.smart_sleep(self.INTERVAL_RETRY)
                        
            except Exception as e:
                # 网络异常也算作失败
                self.failed_attempts += 1
                print(f"[Heartbeat] Exception: {e}")
                
                if self.failed_attempts >= self.MAX_RETRIES:
                    self.sig_auth_failed.emit(f"Network exception: {str(e)}")
                    break
                else:
                    self.smart_sleep(self.INTERVAL_RETRY)

    def smart_sleep(self, seconds):
        """
        分段休眠，确保程序关闭时线程能迅速响应退出，而不是傻等30分钟
        """
        for _ in range(seconds):
            if not self._is_running:
                break
            time.sleep(1)

    def stop(self):
        self._is_running = False
        self.wait()

# 🖥️ 主窗口逻辑
# ============================================================================
class MainWindowLogic(QObject):
    def __init__(self, keyauth_app=None):
        print(">>> 正在初始化 MainWindowLogic，参数:", keyauth_app) # 👈 加这句测试
        super().__init__()
        
        # 1. 加载 UI
        self.ui = self.load_ui_file()
        
        # 2. 初始化核心组件
        self.manager = InstanceManager()
        self.current_instance_id = None
        self.config_widgets = [] 
        
        # 连接管理器信号
        self.manager.sig_instance_list_changed.connect(self.refresh_instances)
        
        # 3. 初始化托盘图标
        self.init_system_tray()
        
        # 3.5 重构侧边栏按钮布局 (2x2)
        self.rearrange_sidebar_buttons()

        # 4. 连接信号
        self.ui.list_instances.itemClicked.connect(self.on_instance_selected)
        self.ui.btn_add_instance.clicked.connect(self.on_add_instance)
        self.ui.btn_refresh_adb.clicked.connect(self.on_refresh_adb)
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.clicked.connect(self.on_toggle_running)
        self.ui.btn_global_settings.clicked.connect(self.on_global_settings)
        self.ui.btn_minimize.clicked.connect(self.minimize_to_tray)
        
        # 5. 初始化右键菜单
        self.setup_list_context_menus()
        self.setup_sidebar_context_menu()
        
        # 6. 初始化界面
        self.refresh_instances()
        self.ui.stacked_pages.setCurrentWidget(self.ui.page_welcome)
        
        # 7. 启动定时器刷新状态
        self.status_timer = QTimer(self)
        self.status_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_ui_status)
        self.status_timer.start(1000) 

        # 8. 安装事件过滤器 (用于监听体力条双击)
        self.ui.installEventFilter(self)
        # 👇 【关键】给体力条和它的文字标签也安装过滤器，确保点哪里都能触发
        self.ui.progress_stamina.installEventFilter(self)
        self.ui.label_stamina_val.installEventFilter(self)

        # 8.5 动态添加 Stats 页面的控件
        self.setup_stats_page()

        # 9. 应用初始翻译
        self.apply_translations()

        # 10. 启动 KeyAuth 心跳线程
        self.keyauth_thread = None
        if keyauth_app:
            self.start_heartbeat(keyauth_app)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 💓 心跳相关逻辑
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def start_heartbeat(self, keyauth_app):
        self.keyauth_thread = KeyAuthHeartbeatThread(keyauth_app)
        # 连接到新的紧急处理槽函数
        self.keyauth_thread.sig_auth_failed.connect(self.on_auth_failed_emergency)
        self.keyauth_thread.start()
        print("[System] Security heartbeat started (Interval: 30m, Redundancy: 3x3m).")

    def on_auth_failed_emergency(self, reason):
        """
        当心跳彻底失败（重试3次后）触发的紧急处理逻辑
        """
        print(f"[Security] EMERGENCY STOP: {reason}")
        
        # A. 停止所有运行中的实例
        running_count = 0
        all_schedulers = self.manager.schedulers
        
        for instance_id, sched in all_schedulers.items():
            if sched.isRunning():
                # 强制停止调度器
                sched.stop_scheduler()
                running_count += 1
                
                # 在对应实例的日志里写一句
                store = self.manager.get_store(instance_id)
                if store:
                    store.push_log("🛑 授权验证失败，强制停止任务！", "ERROR")

        # B. 更新 UI 状态 (调用现有的 update_ui_status 来刷新按钮状态)
        self.update_ui_status()
        
        # C. 禁用核心按钮，防止用户再次点击启动
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setEnabled(False)
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setText("授权失效")
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setStyleSheet("background-color: gray; color: white;")
        
        # D. 弹出对话框报错 (保持窗口开启)
        QMessageBox.critical(
            self.ui, 
            "Authorization Failed", 
            f"安全检测失败，已强制停止所有任务。\n\n原因: {reason}\n\n请检查网络连接或重启软件。"
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 🧩 界面布局与初始化
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def setup_stats_page(self):
        """在 Stats 页面动态添加控件"""
        # 查找 group_stamina 的父 Widget
        parent_widget = self.ui.group_stamina.parent()
        layout = parent_widget.layout()
        
        if not layout:
            layout = QVBoxLayout(parent_widget)
            
        # 创建一个新的 Widget 用于显示侧边栏信息 (不再使用 GroupBox)
        self.group_sidebar = QWidget(parent_widget)
        self.group_sidebar.setObjectName("group_sidebar")
        vbox = QVBoxLayout(self.group_sidebar)
        vbox.setContentsMargins(0, 10, 0, 10) # 增加上下间距
        
        # 创建 Label
        self.label_idle_queues_title = QLabel(self.group_sidebar)
        self.label_idle_queues_val = QLabel("?", self.group_sidebar)
        self.label_idle_queues_val.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: green; font-size: 12pt;")
        
        # 使用 Grid Layout
        hbox = QGridLayout()
        hbox.addWidget(self.label_idle_queues_title, 0, 0)
        hbox.addWidget(self.label_idle_queues_val, 0, 1)
        hbox.setColumnStretch(1, 1)
        
        vbox.addLayout(hbox)
        
        # 添加到主布局 (在 group_stamina 后面)
        layout.addWidget(self.group_sidebar)

        # 添加英雄管理组件
        self.hero_widget = HeroManagementWidget(parent_widget)
        layout.addWidget(self.hero_widget)

        # 添加弹簧，确保内容靠上
        layout.addStretch()

    def apply_translations(self):

        self.ui.btn_global_settings.setText(get_text("btn_global_settings"))
        
        # Welcome
        self.ui.label_welcome.setText(get_text("lbl_welcome"))
        
        # Header
        self.update_ui_status() # 状态文本会在 update_ui_status 里更新
        
        # Tabs
        self.ui.tabWidget.setTabText(0, get_text("tab_tasks"))
        self.ui.tabWidget.setTabText(1, get_text("tab_logs"))
        self.ui.tabWidget.setTabText(2, get_text("tab_stats"))
        
        # Stats Group
        if hasattr(self.ui, 'group_stamina'):
            self.ui.group_stamina.setTitle(get_text("group_stamina"))
            
        if hasattr(self, 'group_sidebar'):
            self.label_idle_queues_title.setText(get_text("lbl_idle_queues"))

        # Task List Titles

            self.ui.lbl_running_title.setText(get_text("lbl_running_title"))

            self.ui.lbl_queue_title.setText(get_text("lbl_queue_title"))
        if hasattr(self.ui, 'lbl_waiting_title'):
            self.ui.lbl_waiting_title.setText(get_text("lbl_waiting_title"))

    def show(self):
        self.ui.show()

    def minimize_to_tray(self):
        self.ui.hide()
        self.tray_icon.showMessage(
            "KSAS 已隐藏",
            "程序仍在后台运行，点击托盘图标可恢复。",
            QSystemTrayIcon.Information,
            2000
        )

    def load_ui_file(self):
        loader = QUiLoader()
        ui_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "main_window.ui")
        ui_file = QFile(ui_path)
        if not ui_file.open(QIODevice.ReadOnly):
            print(f"Cannot open {ui_path}: {ui_file.errorString()}")
            sys.exit(-1)
        ui = loader.load(ui_file)
        ui_file.close()
        if not ui:
            print(loader.errorString())
            sys.exit(-1)
        return ui

    def init_system_tray(self):
        self.tray_icon = QSystemTrayIcon(self.ui)
        icon_path = os.path.join("assets", "logo.png")
        if os.path.exists(icon_path):
            icon = QIcon(icon_path)
            self.tray_icon.setIcon(icon)
            self.ui.setWindowIcon(icon)
            if hasattr(self.ui, 'label_logo'):
                pixmap = QPixmap(icon_path)
                self.ui.label_logo.setPixmap(pixmap.scaled(
                    self.ui.label_logo.size(), 
                    Qt.KeepAspectRatio, 
                    Qt.SmoothTransformation
                ))
                self.ui.label_logo.setText("")
        else:
            self.tray_icon.setIcon(self.ui.style().standardIcon(QStyle.StandardPixmap.SP_ComputerIcon))

        tray_menu = QMenu()
        action_show = QAction(get_text("显示主界面"), self.ui) 
        action_show.triggered.connect(self.show_window)
        tray_menu.addAction(action_show)
        action_quit = QAction(get_text("退出程序"), self.ui) 
        action_quit.triggered.connect(self.quit_app)
        tray_menu.addAction(action_quit)
        
        self.tray_icon.setContextMenu(tray_menu)
        self.tray_icon.activated.connect(self.on_tray_icon_activated)
        self.tray_icon.show()

    def rearrange_sidebar_buttons(self):
        """将侧边栏底部的4个按钮改为 2x2 网格布局"""
        # 1. 获取容器和旧布局
        container = self.ui.sidebar_tools
        old_layout = container.layout()
        
        # 2. 获取按钮对象 (它们已经在 ui 对象里了)
        btn_add = self.ui.btn_add_instance
        btn_refresh = self.ui.btn_refresh_adb
        btn_settings = self.ui.btn_global_settings
        btn_minimize = self.ui.btn_minimize
        
        # 3. 创建新布局
        new_layout = QGridLayout()
        new_layout.setSpacing(5)
        new_layout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        
        # 4. 添加按钮 (Row, Col)
        # 第一行: 添加 | 刷新
        new_layout.addWidget(btn_add, 0, 0)
        new_layout.addWidget(btn_refresh, 0, 1)
        # 第二行: 设置 | 最小化
        new_layout.addWidget(btn_settings, 1, 0)
        new_layout.addWidget(btn_minimize, 1, 1)
        
        # 5. 替换布局
        QWidget().setLayout(old_layout) # 把旧布局的所有权转移给一个临时对象，从而销毁它
        container.setLayout(new_layout)

    # ===========================================
    # 🖱️ 事件过滤器 (处理双击事件等)
    # ===========================================
    def eventFilter(self, obj, event):
        # 1. 拦截关闭事件 -> 最小化
        if obj == self.ui and event.type() == QEvent.Close:
            if global_config.get("close_to_tray", False):
                event.ignore()
                self.ui.hide()
                self.tray_icon.showMessage("KSAS 已隐藏", "点击托盘图标可恢复。", QSystemTrayIcon.Information, 2000)
                return True
            else:
                self.quit_app()
                return False
        
        # 2. 拦截体力条的双击事件 -> 修改体力
        if event.type() == QEvent.MouseButtonDblClick:
            if obj == self.ui.progress_stamina or obj == self.ui.label_stamina_val:
                self.do_edit_stamina()
                return True

        return super().eventFilter(obj, event)

    def do_edit_stamina(self):
        """弹出对话框修改体力"""
        if not self.current_instance_id: return
        
        store = self.manager.get_store(self.current_instance_id)
        current_val = store.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        
        val, ok = QInputDialog.getInt(
            self.ui, 
            get_text("group_stamina"),
            get_text("msg_input_stamina"),
            value=current_val,
            minValue=0, 
            maxValue=10000
        )
        
        if ok:
            store.set_stat("stamina", val)
            self.ui.text_log.append(f"<font color='#FF8C00'>[User] 手动更新体力: {val}</font>")
            # UI 刷新会由 update_ui_status 自动接管，不需要手动 setProgressBar

    def show_window(self):
        self.ui.showNormal()
        self.ui.activateWindow()

    def quit_app(self):
        self.tray_icon.hide()
        # 停止心跳线程
        if hasattr(self, 'keyauth_thread') and self.keyauth_thread:
            self.keyauth_thread.stop()
        sys.exit(0)

    def on_tray_icon_activated(self, reason):
        if reason == QSystemTrayIcon.Trigger:
            if self.ui.isVisible():
                self.ui.hide()
            else:
                self.show_window()

    def refresh_instances(self):
        self.ui.list_instances.clear()
        instances = self.manager.get_all_instances()
        for inst in instances:
            item = QListWidgetItem()
            name = inst.get('alias') or inst.get('id')
            item.setText(name)
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, inst['id'])
            self.ui.list_instances.addItem(item)

    def on_instance_selected(self, item):
        instance_id = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        
        self.current_instance_id = instance_id
        self.ui.stacked_pages.setCurrentWidget(self.ui.page_instance_detail)
        
        all_insts = self.manager.get_all_instances()
        target = next((x for x in all_insts if x['id'] == instance_id), None)
        if target:
            self.ui.label_instance_name.setText(target.get('alias', instance_id))
            
        self.load_task_configs(instance_id)
        
        store = self.manager.get_store(instance_id)
        try: store.sig_log_msg.disconnect()
        except: pass
        store.sig_log_msg.connect(self.append_log)

        # 初始化并刷新英雄管理组件
        if hasattr(self, 'hero_widget'):
            hero_mgr = HeroManager(store)
            self.hero_widget.set_manager(hero_mgr)
        
        self.ui.text_log.clear()
        log_path = os.path.join("data", instance_id, "log", "ksas.log") 
        if os.path.exists(log_path):
            try:
                with open(log_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    lines = f.readlines()[-50:]
                    for line in lines:
                        self.ui.text_log.append(f"<span style='color:gray'>{line.strip()}</span>")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"读取日志失败: {e}")
        self.update_ui_status()

    def append_log(self, level, full_msg):
        try:
            if "|||" in full_msg:
                sender_id, real_msg = full_msg.split("|||", 1)
            else:
                sender_id = "System"
                real_msg = full_msg
                
            if sender_id != self.current_instance_id:
                return
                
            color = "black"
            if "ERROR" in level: color = "red"
            elif "WARNING" in level: color = "#FF8C00"
            elif "DEBUG" in level: color = "gray"
            
            time_str = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
            html = f'<span style="color:gray">[{time_str}]</span> <span style="color:{color}">{real_msg}</span>'
            
            doc = self.ui.text_log.document()
            while doc.blockCount() > 100:
                cursor = self.ui.text_log.textCursor()
                cursor.movePosition(QTextCursor.Start)
                cursor.select(QTextCursor.BlockUnderCursor)
                cursor.removeSelectedText()
                cursor.deleteChar()

            self.ui.text_log.append(html)
            self.ui.text_log.ensureCursorVisible()
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Log Error: {e}")

    def load_task_configs(self, instance_id):
        layout = self.ui.scrollAreaWidgetContents.layout()
        while layout.count():
            child = layout.takeAt(0)
            if child.widget():
                child.widget().deleteLater()
        self.config_widgets = []
        
        store = self.manager.get_store(instance_id)
        task_classes = get_all_tasks()
        
        # 动态分类
        categories = {}
        
        # 默认分类映射 (作为后备)
        default_category_map = {
            "GeneralSettings": "General",
            "WolfTask": "Combat",
            "BeastTask": "Combat",
            "RallyTask": "Combat",
            "IntelTask": "Daily",
            "GatherTask": "Daily",
            "ShopTask": "Shop",
            "TestSidebarTask": "Other",
            "ExampleTask": "Other"
        }

        for task_cls in task_classes:
            # 1. 尝试从 config_schema 获取 menu
            menu = "Other"
            if hasattr(task_cls, 'config_schema'):
                for item in task_cls.config_schema:
                    if 'menu' in item:
                        menu = item['menu']
                        break
            
            # 2. 如果没找到，尝试使用默认映射
            if menu == "Other":
                menu = default_category_map.get(task_cls.__name__, "Other")
            
            if menu not in categories:
                categories[menu] = []
            categories[menu].append(task_cls)

        # 排序分类: General 总是第一，Other 总是最后，其他按字母顺序
        sorted_cats = sorted(categories.keys())
        if "General" in sorted_cats:
            sorted_cats.remove("General")
            sorted_cats.insert(0, "General")
        if "Other" in sorted_cats:
            sorted_cats.remove("Other")
            sorted_cats.append("Other")
        
        for cat_name in sorted_cats:
            tasks = categories[cat_name]
            if not tasks: continue
            
            # 获取翻译后的分类名称
            trans_key = f"cat_{cat_name.lower()}"
            display_name = get_text(trans_key)
            if display_name == trans_key:
                display_name = cat_name

            # 创建容器布局
            if cat_name == "General":
                # General 不折叠，直接添加
                container_layout = layout
            else:
                # 其他分类使用折叠框
                box = CollapsibleBox(title=display_name)
                layout.addWidget(box)
                
                # 折叠框的内容布局
                content_widget = QWidget()
                vbox = QVBoxLayout(content_widget)
                vbox.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
                box.setContentLayout(vbox)
                container_layout = vbox

                # 当分类折叠时，自动折叠所有子任务
                box.toggled_state.connect(lambda c, l=vbox: self.on_category_toggled(c, l))
            
            for task_cls in tasks:
                widget = TaskWidgetFactory.create_widget(task_cls)
                widget.load_from_store(store)
                container_layout.addWidget(widget)
                self.config_widgets.append(widget)
                widget.show()

        layout.addStretch()
        self.ui.scrollAreaWidgetContents.update()

    def on_category_toggled(self, checked, layout):
        """当分类折叠时，折叠其下的所有任务卡片"""
        if not checked: # Collapsed
            for i in range(layout.count()):
                item = layout.itemAt(i)
                if item.widget() and hasattr(item.widget(), 'collapse'):
                    item.widget().collapse()

    def save_current_configs(self):
        if not self.current_instance_id:
            return
        store = self.manager.get_store(self.current_instance_id)
        for widget in self.config_widgets:
            widget.save_to_store(store)

    def on_toggle_running(self):
        if not self.current_instance_id:
            return
        self.save_current_configs()
        
        sched = self.manager.get_scheduler(self.current_instance_id)
        if not sched:
            return

        if self.ui.btn_toggle_running.isChecked():
            sched.start_scheduler()
            self.ui.text_log.append(f"<font color='green'><b>>>> 调度器已启动 (Scheduler Started)</b></font>")
        else:
            sched.stop_scheduler()
            self.ui.text_log.append(f"<font color='red'><b>>>> 调度器正在停止...</b></font>")
        self.update_ui_status()

    def update_ui_status(self):
        if not self.current_instance_id:
            return
        
        sched = self.manager.get_scheduler(self.current_instance_id)
        if not sched: return
        
        is_running = sched.isRunning()
        # 更新按钮状态（如果代码与按钮状态不一致时）
        if self.ui.btn_toggle_running.isChecked() != is_running:
            # 在紧急停止情况下，这里可能会把按钮重置为 unchecked，这符合预期
            self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setChecked(is_running)
            
        if is_running:
            self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setText(get_text("btn_stop"))
            self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setProperty("state", "running")
            self.ui.label_status_text.setText(get_text("lbl_status_running"))
            self.ui.label_status_icon.setText("🟢")
        else:
            # 如果是因为授权失败被禁用，文字会被 on_auth_failed_emergency 覆盖，这里需要保留
            if self.ui.btn_toggle_running.isEnabled():
                self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setText(get_text("btn_start"))
            
            self.ui.btn_toggle_running.setProperty("state", "stopped")
            self.ui.label_status_text.setText(get_text("lbl_status_idle"))
            self.ui.label_status_icon.setText("⚪")
        
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.style().unpolish(self.ui.btn_toggle_running)
        self.ui.btn_toggle_running.style().polish(self.ui.btn_toggle_running)

        store = self.manager.get_store(self.current_instance_id)
        stamina = store.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        self.ui.progress_stamina.setMaximum(200)
        self.ui.progress_stamina.setValue(int(stamina))
        self.ui.progress_stamina.setTextVisible(False)
        self.ui.label_stamina_val.setText(f"{stamina} / 200")

        # 更新侧边栏统计
        idle_queues = store.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", "?")
        if hasattr(self, 'label_idle_queues_val'):
            self.label_idle_queues_val.setText(str(idle_queues))

        self.update_task_lists(sched)

    def update_task_lists(self, sched):
        self.ui.list_running.clear()
        self.ui.list_queue.clear()
        self.ui.list_waiting.clear()
        
        if not sched: return

        store = self.manager.get_store(self.current_instance_id)
        
        current_task_name = store.get_runtime("current_task", "Idle")
        is_running_logic = store.get_runtime("is_running", False)

        if is_running_logic and "休眠" not in current_task_name and "待机" not in current_task_name and "Idle" not in current_task_name:
             self.ui.list_running.addItem(f"{get_text('lbl_running_title')} {current_task_name}")
        elif is_running_logic:
             self.ui.list_running.addItem(f"{get_text('lbl_waiting_title')} {current_task_name}")
        
        now = datetime.now()
        queue_tasks = []
        waiting_tasks = []

        for task in sched.tasks:
            if not task.enabled:
                continue
            
            time_str = task.next_run.strftime("%H:%M:%S") if task.next_run.year > 2000 else "立即"
            
            if task.name == current_task_name and "休眠" not in current_task_name:
                continue

            if now >= task.next_run:
                queue_tasks.append((task, time_str))
            else:
                waiting_tasks.append((task, time_str))

        queue_tasks.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].priority)
        for task, t_str in queue_tasks:
            item = QListWidgetItem(f"[{task.priority}] {task.name}")
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, task.name)
            self.ui.list_queue.addItem(item)

        waiting_tasks.sort(key=lambda x: x[0].next_run)
        for task, t_str in waiting_tasks:
            item = QListWidgetItem(f"{task.name}  ({t_str})")
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, task.name)
            self.ui.list_waiting.addItem(item)

    def setup_list_context_menus(self):
        for lst in [self.ui.list_queue, self.ui.list_waiting]:
            lst.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.CustomContextMenu)
            lst.customContextMenuRequested.connect(self.on_task_context_menu)

    def on_task_context_menu(self, pos):
        sender_list = self.sender()
        item = sender_list.itemAt(pos)
        if not item: return
        
        task_name = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        menu = QMenu()
        action_run = QAction(f"{get_text('menu_run_now')} {task_name}", self.ui)
        action_run.triggered.connect(lambda: self.reset_task_next_run(task_name))
        menu.addAction(action_run)
        menu.exec(QCursor.pos())

    def reset_task_next_run(self, task_name):
        if not self.current_instance_id: return
        store = self.manager.get_store(self.current_instance_id)
        store.set_stat(f"tasks.{task_name}.next_run", "")
        self.ui.text_log.append(f"<font color='#1e90ff'>[User] 已强制插队任务: {task_name}</font>")
    
    def setup_sidebar_context_menu(self):
        self.ui.list_instances.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.CustomContextMenu)
        self.ui.list_instances.customContextMenuRequested.connect(self.on_sidebar_menu)

    def on_sidebar_menu(self, pos):
        item = self.ui.list_instances.itemAt(pos)
        if not item: return
        instance_id = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        
        menu = QMenu()
        action_edit = QAction(get_text("menu_edit_info"), self.ui)
        action_edit.triggered.connect(lambda: self.do_edit_instance(instance_id))
        menu.addAction(action_edit)

        action_folder = QAction(get_text("menu_open_folder"), self.ui)
        action_folder.triggered.connect(lambda: self.do_open_folder(instance_id))
        menu.addAction(action_folder)
        
        menu.addSeparator()
        
        action_del = QAction(get_text("menu_delete"), self.ui)
        action_del.triggered.connect(lambda: self.do_delete_instance(instance_id))
        menu.addAction(action_del)
        menu.exec(QCursor.pos())

    def do_edit_instance(self, instance_id):
        store = self.manager.get_store(instance_id)
        all_insts = self.manager.get_all_instances()
        target = next((x for x in all_insts if x['id'] == instance_id), None)
        
        old_name = target['name'] if target else instance_id
        old_serial = store.get_cfg("meta.serial", "")
        old_diana = store.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        old_march = store.get_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", 5)
        
        dialog = EditInstanceDialog(
            self.ui,
            current_name=old_name,
            current_serial=old_serial,
            current_diana=old_diana,
            current_march_slots=old_march
        )
        
        if dialog.exec():
            new_name, new_serial, new_diana, new_march = dialog.get_data()
            if not new_name or not new_serial:
                QMessageBox.warning(self.ui, get_text("msg_error"), get_text("msg_input_invalid"))
                return

            self.manager.update_instance(
                instance_id,
                new_serial=new_serial,
                new_alias=new_name,
                new_diana_level=new_diana,
                new_march_slots=new_march
            )
            self.ui.text_log.append(f"<font color='blue'>[System] 实例信息已更新: {new_name} (Diana Lv.{new_diana}, March: {new_march})</font>")

    def do_delete_instance(self, instance_id):
        all_insts = self.manager.get_all_instances()
        target = next((x for x in all_insts if x['id'] == instance_id), None)
        name = target['name'] if target else instance_id

        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self.ui, 
            get_text("msg_confirm_del"),
            get_text("msg_del_warn", name),
            QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No,
            QMessageBox.No
        )
        
        if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:
            sched = self.manager.get_scheduler(instance_id)
            if sched and sched.isRunning():
                sched.stop_scheduler()
                
            self.manager.delete_instance(instance_id)
            
            if self.current_instance_id == instance_id:
                self.current_instance_id = None
                self.ui.stacked_pages.setCurrentIndex(0) 
            
            QMessageBox.information(self.ui, get_text("msg_success"), get_text("msg_success"))

    def do_open_folder(self, instance_id):
        store = self.manager.get_store(instance_id)
        if store:
            path = os.path.abspath(store.base_dir)
            os.startfile(path)

    def _format_number(self, num):
        try:
            n = float(num)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return "0"
        if n >= 1_000_000:
            return f"{n/1_000_000:.1f}M"
        elif n >= 1_000:
            return f"{n/1_000:.1f}K"
        else:
            return str(int(n))

    def on_add_instance(self):
        dialog = AddInstanceDialog(self.ui)
        if dialog.exec():
            name, serial, diana, march = dialog.get_data()
            if not name or not serial:
                QMessageBox.warning(self.ui, get_text("msg_error"), get_text("msg_input_invalid"))
                return
            
            success, msg = self.manager.create_instance(name, serial, diana, march)
            if success:
                QMessageBox.information(self.ui, get_text("msg_success"), f"Instance '{name}' created!")
            else:
                QMessageBox.critical(self.ui, get_text("msg_error"), f"Failed: {msg}")

    def on_refresh_adb(self):
        self.refresh_instances()
        self.ui.statusbar.showMessage("实例列表已刷新", 2000)

    def on_global_settings(self):
        dialog = SettingsDialog(self.ui)
        if dialog.exec():
            # 如果设置里改了语言，这里需要刷新界面
            self.apply_translations()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import sys
import shutil
import zipfile
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QTabWidget, QWidget, 
    QLabel, QCheckBox, QComboBox, QLineEdit, QPushButton, 
    QFileDialog, QMessageBox, QGroupBox, QFormLayout
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, Signal
from config import global_config
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class SettingsDialog(QDialog):
    sig_config_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(get_text("btn_global_settings"))
        self.resize(500, 400)
        self.setup_ui()
        self.load_settings()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

        # Tab 1: 通用设置
        self.tab_general = QWidget()
        self.setup_general_tab()
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_general, get_text("tab_general"))

        # Tab 2: ADB 设置
        self.tab_adb = QWidget()
        self.setup_adb_tab()
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_adb, get_text("tab_adb"))

        # Tab 3: 数据管理
        self.tab_data = QWidget()
        self.setup_data_tab()
        self.tabs.addTab(self.tab_data, get_text("tab_data"))

        # 底部按钮
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_save = QPushButton(get_text("btn_save"))
        btn_save.clicked.connect(self.save_settings)
        btn_cancel = QPushButton(get_text("btn_cancel"))
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_save)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def setup_general_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.tab_general)
        
        # 语言
        group_lang = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_language"))
        form_lang = QFormLayout()
        self.combo_lang = QComboBox()
        self.combo_lang.addItems(["简体中文 (zh_CN)", "English (en_US)"])
        form_lang.addRow(get_text("grp_language") + ":", self.combo_lang)
        group_lang.setLayout(form_lang)
        layout.addWidget(group_lang)

        # 行为
        group_behavior = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_behavior"))
        vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        self.chk_close_tray = QCheckBox(get_text("chk_close_tray"))
        self.chk_auto_connect = QCheckBox(get_text("chk_auto_connect"))
        vbox.addWidget(self.chk_close_tray)
        vbox.addWidget(self.chk_auto_connect)
        group_behavior.setLayout(vbox)
        layout.addWidget(group_behavior)

        layout.addStretch()

    def setup_adb_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.tab_adb)

        group_path = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_adb_path"))
        vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        
        self.chk_internal_adb = QCheckBox(get_text("chk_internal_adb"))
        self.chk_internal_adb.toggled.connect(self.on_internal_adb_toggled)
        vbox.addWidget(self.chk_internal_adb)

        hbox = QHBoxLayout()
        self.line_adb_path = QLineEdit()
        self.line_adb_path.setPlaceholderText("Select adb.exe path...")
        btn_browse = QPushButton(get_text("btn_browse"))
        btn_browse.clicked.connect(self.browse_adb)
        hbox.addWidget(self.line_adb_path)
        hbox.addWidget(btn_browse)
        vbox.addLayout(hbox)
        
        group_path.setLayout(vbox)
        layout.addWidget(group_path)

        # 工具
        group_tools = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_adb_tools"))
        hbox_tools = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_restart_adb = QPushButton(get_text("btn_restart_adb"))
        btn_restart_adb.clicked.connect(self.restart_adb_server)
        hbox_tools.addWidget(btn_restart_adb)
        group_tools.setLayout(hbox_tools)
        layout.addWidget(group_tools)

        layout.addStretch()

    def setup_data_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.tab_data)

        group_backup = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_backup"))
        vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        
        btn_backup = QPushButton(get_text("btn_backup"))
        btn_backup.clicked.connect(self.backup_data)
        
        btn_restore = QPushButton(get_text("btn_restore"))
        btn_restore.clicked.connect(self.restore_data)

        vbox.addWidget(btn_backup)
        vbox.addWidget(btn_restore)
        group_backup.setLayout(vbox)
        layout.addWidget(group_backup)

        group_explore = QGroupBox(get_text("grp_explorer"))
        hbox = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_open_data = QPushButton(get_text("btn_open_data"))
        btn_open_data.clicked.connect(lambda: self.open_folder("data"))
        hbox.addWidget(btn_open_data)
        group_explore.setLayout(hbox)
        layout.addWidget(group_explore)

        layout.addStretch()

    # ================= Logic =================

    def load_settings(self):
        # Language
        lang = global_config.get("language", "zh_CN")
        idx = 0 if lang == "zh_CN" else 1
        self.combo_lang.setCurrentIndex(idx)

        # Behavior
        self.chk_close_tray.setChecked(global_config.get("close_to_tray", True))
        self.chk_auto_connect.setChecked(global_config.get("auto_connect", True))

        # ADB
        use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
        self.chk_internal_adb.setChecked(use_internal)
        self.line_adb_path.setText(global_config.get("adb_path", ""))
        self.line_adb_path.setEnabled(not use_internal)

    def save_settings(self):
        # Language
        lang = "zh_CN" if self.combo_lang.currentIndex() == 0 else "en_US"
        global_config.set("language", lang)

        # Behavior
        global_config.set("close_to_tray", self.chk_close_tray.isChecked())
        global_config.set("auto_connect", self.chk_auto_connect.isChecked())

        # ADB
        global_config.set("use_internal_adb", self.chk_internal_adb.isChecked())
        global_config.set("adb_path", self.line_adb_path.text().strip())

        global_config.save()
        self.sig_config_changed.emit()
        self.accept()

    def on_internal_adb_toggled(self, checked):
        self.line_adb_path.setEnabled(not checked)

    def browse_adb(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "选择 ADB", "", "Executable (*.exe);;All Files (*)")
        if path:
            self.line_adb_path.setText(path)

    def restart_adb_server(self):
        try:
            subprocess.run(["adb", "kill-server"], check=False)
            subprocess.run(["adb", "start-server"], check=False)
            QMessageBox.information(self, get_text("msg_success"), get_text("msg_adb_restarted"))
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, get_text("msg_error"), f"Failed: {e}")

    def backup_data(self):
        try:
            # 确保 backup 文件夹存在
            backup_dir = "backup"
            if not os.path.exists(backup_dir):
                os.makedirs(backup_dir)

            timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            filename = os.path.join(backup_dir, f"backup_{timestamp}.zip")
            
            with zipfile.ZipFile(filename, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:
                if os.path.exists("data"):
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk("data"):
                        for file in files:
                            # 排除截图文件夹，太大了
                            if "snapshots" in root:
                                continue
                            file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                            arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, ".")
                            zipf.write(file_path, arcname)
            
            QMessageBox.information(self, get_text("msg_success"), get_text("msg_backup_success").format(os.path.abspath(filename)))
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, get_text("msg_error"), str(e))

    def restore_data(self):
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select Backup", "", "Zip Files (*.zip)")
        if not path:
            return

        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Confirm Restore",
            get_text("msg_restore_confirm"),
            QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No
        )
        if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:
            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(path, 'r') as zipf:
                    zipf.extractall(".")
                QMessageBox.information(self, get_text("msg_success"), get_text("msg_restore_success"))
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, get_text("msg_error"), str(e))

    def open_folder(self, folder):
        path = os.path.abspath(folder)
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            os.makedirs(path)
        os.startfile(path)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QCheckBox, QLabel, QSpinBox, QGroupBox, QFormLayout, QDoubleSpinBox, QComboBox
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from ui.collapsible_box import CollapsibleBox

class BaseTaskConfigWidget(CollapsibleBox):
    """
    所有任务配置小部件的基类。
    自动处理 "启用/禁用" (CollapsibleBox 的 checkable 属性)。
    """
    def __init__(self, task_class, parent=None):
        if task_class.__name__ == "GeneralSettings":
            self.task_name = "General"
        else:
            self.task_name = task_class.__name__

        super().__init__(title=self.task_name, parent=parent, checkable=True)
        self.task_class = task_class



        
        self.setChecked(False) # 默认关闭
        
        # 内部布局
        self.content_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.layout = self.content_layout
        self.setup_ui()
        self.setContentLayout(self.content_layout)
        
    def setup_ui(self):
        """
        【子类重写】在 self.layout 里添加具体的控件
        """
        pass
        
    def load_from_store(self, store):
        """
        从 DataStore 读取配置并刷新 UI
        """
        # 1. 读取通用开关
        # Key 格式: modules.exampletask.enable
        enabled = store.get_cfg(f"modules.{self.task_name.lower()}.enable", False)
        self.setChecked(enabled)
        
        # 2. 读取子类特定配置
        self.load_specific_config(store)
        
        # 3. 连接信号 (如果尚未连接)
        try:
            store.sig_config_changed.disconnect(self.on_config_changed)
        except:
            pass
        store.sig_config_changed.connect(self.on_config_changed)

    def on_config_changed(self, key, value):
        """
        当配置发生变化时（例如后台任务修改了剩余次数），自动刷新 UI
        """
        target_prefix = f"modules.{self.task_name.lower()}"
        if target_prefix in key:
            store = self.sender()
            if store:
                # 如果是 enable 发生变化，更新 checkbox
                if key == f"{target_prefix}.enable":
                    self.setChecked(value)
                
                # 刷新其他配置
                self.load_specific_config(store)

    def save_to_store(self, store):
        """
        将 UI 当前状态保存到 DataStore
        """
        # 1. 保存通用开关
        store.set_cfg(f"modules.{self.task_name.lower()}.enable", self.isChecked())
        
        # 2. 保存子类特定配置
        self.save_specific_config(store)

    def load_specific_config(self, store):
        """【子类重写】读取特定参数"""
        pass

    def save_specific_config(self, store):
        """【子类重写】保存特定参数"""
        pass
